NO MOCK DATA.\n6\t */\n7\t\n8\timport { Module } from '@nestjs/common';\n9\timport { ScheduleModule } from '@nestjs/schedule';\n10\timport { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\n11\t\n12\timport { AuditLogModule } from '../audit/audit.module';\n13\t\n14\timport { AnalyticsController } from './controllers/analytics.controller';\n15\timport { ReportsController } from './controllers/reports.controller';\n16\timport { AnalyticsSnapshot, ReportDefinition, ReportExecution } from './entities/analytics-snapshot.entity';\n17\timport { InvoiceReadOnly } from './entities/external/invoice.entity';\n18\timport { SubscriptionReadOnly } from './entities/external/subscription.entity';\n19\timport { TenantReadOnly } from './entities/external/tenant.entity';\n20\timport { UserReadOnly } from './entities/external/user.entity';\n21\timport { AnalyticsSnapshotScheduler } from './services/analytics-snapshot.scheduler';\n22\timport { AnalyticsService } from './services/analytics.service';\n23\timport { ReportsService } from './services/reports.service';\n24\t\n25\t@Module({\n26\t  imports: [\n27\t    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([\n28\t      AnalyticsSnapshot,\n29\t      ReportDefinition,\n30\t      ReportExecution,\n31\t      // External entities for cross-service analytics\n32\t      // These are read-only - source of truth is in their respective services\n33\t      TenantReadOnly,\n34\t      UserReadOnly,\n35\t      SubscriptionReadOnly,\n36\t      InvoiceReadOnly,\n37\t    ]),\n38\t    ScheduleModule,\n39\t    AuditLogModule,\n40\t  ],\n41\t  controllers: [AnalyticsController, ReportsController],\n42\t  providers: [AnalyticsService, ReportsService, AnalyticsSnapshotScheduler],\n43\t  exports: [AnalyticsService, ReportsService],\n44\t})\n45\texport class AnalyticsModule {\n46\t  readonly moduleName = AnalyticsModule.name;\n47\t}\n48\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"5a901d0d-03d5-498d-86ba-e51844a507c4","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:19:50.827Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/apps/admin-api-service/src/analytics/analytics.module.ts","content":"/**\n * Analytics Module\n *\n * Dashboard KPI, metrik hesaplama ve rapor oluşturma modülü.\n * Uses real database queries to calculate metrics - NO MOCK DATA.\n */\n\nimport { Module } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ScheduleModule } from '@nestjs/schedule';\nimport { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\n\nimport { AuditLogModule } from '../audit/audit.module';\n\nimport { AnalyticsController } from './controllers/analytics.controller';\nimport { ReportsController } from './controllers/reports.controller';\nimport { AnalyticsSnapshot, ReportDefinition, ReportExecution } from './entities/analytics-snapshot.entity';\nimport { InvoiceReadOnly } from './entities/external/invoice.entity';\nimport { SubscriptionReadOnly } from './entities/external/subscription.entity';\nimport { TenantReadOnly } from './entities/external/tenant.entity';\nimport { UserReadOnly } from './entities/external/user.entity';\nimport { AnalyticsSnapshotScheduler } from './services/analytics-snapshot.scheduler';\nimport { AnalyticsService } from './services/analytics.service';\nimport { ReportsService } from './services/reports.service';\n\n@Module({\n  imports: [\n    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([\n      AnalyticsSnapshot,\n      ReportDefinition,\n      ReportExecution,\n      // External entities for cross-service analytics\n      // These are read-only - source of truth is in their respective services\n      TenantReadOnly,\n      UserReadOnly,\n      SubscriptionReadOnly,\n      InvoiceReadOnly,\n    ]),\n    ScheduleModule,\n    AuditLogModule,\n  ],\n  controllers: [AnalyticsController, ReportsController],\n  providers: [AnalyticsService, ReportsService, AnalyticsSnapshotScheduler],\n  exports: [AnalyticsService, ReportsService],\n})\nexport class AnalyticsModule {\n  readonly moduleName = AnalyticsModule.name;\n}\n","numLines":48,"startLine":1,"totalLines":48}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UCo2LM1LYXgwRNiqPTtEeQ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Analytics Controller\n3\t *\n4\t * Dashboard KPI ve metrik endpoint'leri.\n5\t */\n6\t\n7\timport {\n8\t  Controller,\n9\t  Get,\n10\t  Query,\n11\t  BadRequestException,\n12\t} from '@nestjs/common';\n13\timport { ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';\n14\t\n15\timport {\n16\t  AnalyticsGranularity,\n17\t  AnalyticsRange,\n18\t  TimeSeriesData,\n19\t  TimeSeriesPoint,\n20\t  TimeSeriesResponse,\n21\t} from '../entities/analytics-snapshot.entity';\n22\timport { AnalyticsService } from '../services/analytics.service';\n23\t\n24\t// INPUT VALIDATION: Constants for parameter limits\n25\tconst MIN_DATA_POINTS = 1;\n26\tconst MAX_DATA_POINTS = 365;\n27\tconst VALID_PERIODS = ['day', 'week', 'month', 'year'] as const;\n28\tconst VALID_RANGES = ['7d', '30d', '90d', '1y'] as const;\n29\tconst VALID_GRANULARITIES = ['day', 'week', 'month'] as const;\n30\t\n31\ttype AnalyticsTrendResponse = TimeSeriesData | TimeSeriesResponse;\n32\ttype RevenueTrendAnalyticsResponse = Awaited<ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getRevenueTrendAnalytics']>>;\n33\t\n34\t/**\n35\t * Validate and sanitize dataPoints parameter\n36\t * Prevents DoS attacks from extremely large values and ensures valid input\n37\t */\n38\tfunction validateDataPoints(value: unknown): number {\n39\t  const num = typeof value === 'string' ? parseInt(value, 10) : Number(value);\n40\t\n41\t  if (isNaN(num) || num < MIN_DATA_POINTS) {\n42\t    throw new BadRequestException(\n43\t      `dataPoints must be a positive integer (min: ${MIN_DATA_POINTS}, max: ${MAX_DATA_POINTS})`,\n44\t    );\n45\t  }\n46\t\n47\t  return Math.min(num, MAX_DATA_POINTS);\n48\t}\n49\t\n50\t/**\n51\t * Parse period parameter - supports both standard formats and shorthand\n52\t * Standard: 'day', 'week', 'month', 'year'\n53\t * Shorthand: '30d' (30 days), '12m' (12 months), '1y' (1 year), '4w' (4 weeks)\n54\t * Returns { period, dataPoints } where period is the base unit and dataPoints is extracted from shorthand\n55\t */\n56\tfunction parsePeriodParameter(value: string, defaultDataPoints: number): { period: 'day' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year'; dataPoints: number } {\n57\t  // Check if it's a standard period format\n58\t  if (VALID_PERIODS.includes(value as typeof VALID_PERIODS[number])) {\n59\t    return { period: value as 'day' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year', dataPoints: defaultDataPoints };\n60\t  }\n61\t\n62\t  // Try to parse shorthand format (e.g., '30d', '12m', '1y', '4w')\n63\t  const match = value.match(/^(\\d+)([dwmy])$/i);\n64\t  if (match) {\n65\t    const num = parseInt(match[1] || '0', 10);\n66\t    const unit = (match[2] || 'd').toLowerCase();\n67\t\n68\t    if (num > 0 && num <= MAX_DATA_POINTS) {\n69\t      switch (unit) {\n70\t        case 'd':\n71\t          return { period: 'day', dataPoints: num };\n72\t        case 'w':\n73\t          return { period: 'week', dataPoints: num };\n74\t        case 'm':\n75\t          return { period: 'month', dataPoints: num };\n76\t        case 'y':\n77\t          return { period: 'year', dataPoints: num };\n78\t      }\n79\t    }\n80\t  }\n81\t\n82\t  throw new BadRequestException(\n83\t    `period must be one of: ${VALID_PERIODS.join(', ')}, or a shorthand like '30d', '12m', '1y', '4w'`,\n84\t  );\n85\t}\n86\t\n87\tfunction parseRangeParameter(\n88\t  range: string,\n89\t  granularity?: string,\n90\t): { range: AnalyticsRange; granularity: AnalyticsGranularity; period: 'day'; dataPoints: number } {\n91\t  if (!VALID_RANGES.includes(range as AnalyticsRange)) {\n92\t    throw new BadRequestException(`range must be one of: ${VALID_RANGES.join(', ')}`);\n93\t  }\n94\t\n95\t  const typedRange = range as AnalyticsRange;\n96\t  const defaultGranularity: Record<AnalyticsRange, AnalyticsGranularity> = {\n97\t    '7d': 'day',\n98\t    '30d': 'day',\n99\t    '90d': 'week',\n100\t    '1y': 'month',\n101\t  };\n102\t\n103\t  const typedGranularity = granularity\n104\t    ? granularity as AnalyticsGranularity\n105\t    : defaultGranularity[typedRange];\n106\t\n107\t  if (!VALID_GRANULARITIES.includes(typedGranularity)) {\n108\t    throw new BadRequestException(`granularity must be one of: ${VALID_GRANULARITIES.join(', ')}`);\n109\t  }\n110\t\n111\t  const dataPointsByRange: Record<AnalyticsRange, number> = {\n112\t    '7d': 7,\n113\t    '30d': 30,\n114\t    '90d': 90,\n115\t    '1y': 365,\n116\t  };\n117\t\n118\t  return {\n119\t    range: typedRange,\n120\t    granularity: typedGranularity,\n121\t    period: 'day',\n122\t    dataPoints: dataPointsByRange[typedRange],\n123\t  };\n124\t}\n125\t\n126\tfunction toTimeSeriesResponse(\n127\t  timeSeries: TimeSeriesData,\n128\t  range: AnalyticsRange,\n129\t  granularity: AnalyticsGranularity,\n130\t  source: string,\n131\t): TimeSeriesResponse {\n132\t  return {\n133\t    range,\n134\t    granularity,\n135\t    data: aggregateTimeSeriesPoints(timeSeries.data, granularity),\n136\t    source,\n137\t    asOf: new Date().toISOString(),\n138\t  };\n139\t}\n140\t\n141\tfunction aggregateTimeSeriesPoints(\n142\t  points: TimeSeriesPoint[],\n143\t  granularity: * Analytics Controller\n *\n * Dashboard KPI ve metrik endpoint'leri.\n */\n\nimport {\n  Controller,\n  Get,\n  Query,\n  BadRequestException,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';\n\nimport {\n  AnalyticsGranularity,\n  AnalyticsRange,\n  TimeSeriesData,\n  TimeSeriesPoint,\n  TimeSeriesResponse,\n} from '../entities/analytics-snapshot.entity';\nimport { AnalyticsService } from '../services/analytics.service';\n\n// INPUT VALIDATION: Constants for parameter limits\nconst MIN_DATA_POINTS = 1;\nconst MAX_DATA_POINTS = 365;\nconst VALID_PERIODS = ['day', 'week', 'month', 'year'] as const;\nconst VALID_RANGES = ['7d', '30d', '90d', '1y'] as const;\nconst VALID_GRANULARITIES = ['day', 'week', 'month'] as const;\n\ntype AnalyticsTrendResponse = TimeSeriesData | TimeSeriesResponse;\ntype RevenueTrendAnalyticsResponse = Awaited<ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getRevenueTrendAnalytics']>>;\n\n/**\n * Validate and sanitize dataPoints parameter\n * Prevents DoS attacks from extremely large values and ensures valid input\n */\nfunction validateDataPoints(value: unknown): number {\n  const num = typeof value === 'string' ? parseInt(value, 10) : Number(value);\n\n  if (isNaN(num) || num < MIN_DATA_POINTS) {\n    throw new BadRequestException(\n      `dataPoints must be a positive integer (min: ${MIN_DATA_POINTS}, max: ${MAX_DATA_POINTS})`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  return Math.min(num, MAX_DATA_POINTS);\n}\n\n/**\n * Parse period parameter - supports both standard formats and shorthand\n * Standard: 'day', 'week', 'month', 'year'\n * Shorthand: '30d' (30 days), '12m' (12 months), '1y' (1 year), '4w' (4 weeks)\n * Returns { period, dataPoints } where period is the base unit and dataPoints is extracted from shorthand\n */\nfunction parsePeriodParameter(value: string, defaultDataPoints: number): { period: 'day' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year'; dataPoints: number } {\n  // Check if it's a standard period format\n  if (VALID_PERIODS.includes(value as typeof VALID_PERIODS[number])) {\n    return { period: value as 'day' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year', dataPoints: defaultDataPoints };\n  }\n\n  // Try to parse shorthand format (e.g., '30d', '12m', '1y', '4w')\n  const match = value.match(/^(\\d+)([dwmy])$/i);\n  if (match) {\n    const num = parseInt(match[1] || '0', 10);\n    const unit = (match[2] || 'd').toLowerCase();\n\n    if (num > 0 && num <= MAX_DATA_POINTS) {\n      switch (unit) {\n        case 'd':\n          return { period: 'day', dataPoints: num };\n        case 'w':\n          return { period: 'week', dataPoints: num };\n        case 'm':\n          return { period: 'month', dataPoints: num };\n        case 'y':\n          return { period: 'year', dataPoints: num };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  throw new BadRequestException(\n    `period must be one of: ${VALID_PERIODS.join(', ')}, or a shorthand like '30d', '12m', '1y', '4w'`,\n  );\n}\n\nfunction parseRangeParameter(\n  range: string,\n  granularity?: string,\n): { range: AnalyticsRange; granularity: AnalyticsGranularity; period: 'day'; dataPoints: number } {\n  if (!VALID_RANGES.includes(range as AnalyticsRange)) {\n    throw new BadRequestException(`range must be one of: ${VALID_RANGES.join(', ')}`);\n  }\n\n  const typedRange = range as AnalyticsRange;\n  const defaultGranularity: Record<AnalyticsRange, AnalyticsGranularity> = {\n    '7d': 'day',\n    '30d': 'day',\n    '90d': 'week',\n    '1y': 'month',\n  };\n\n  const typedGranularity = granularity\n    ? granularity as AnalyticsGranularity\n    : defaultGranularity[typedRange];\n\n  if (!VALID_GRANULARITIES.includes(typedGranularity)) {\n    throw new BadRequestException(`granularity must be one of: ${VALID_GRANULARITIES.join(', ')}`);\n  }\n\n  const dataPointsByRange: Record<AnalyticsRange, number> = {\n    '7d': 7,\n    '30d': 30,\n    '90d': 90,\n    '1y': 365,\n  };\n\n  return {\n    range: typedRange,\n    granularity: typedGranularity,\n    period: 'day',\n    dataPoints: dataPointsByRange[typedRange],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toTimeSeriesResponse(\n  timeSeries: TimeSeriesData,\n  range: AnalyticsRange,\n  granularity: AnalyticsGranularity,\n  source: string,\n): TimeSeriesResponse {\n  return {\n    range,\n    granularity,\n    data: aggregateTimeSeriesPoints(timeSeries.data, granularity),\n    source,\n    asOf: new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction aggregateTimeSeriesPoints(\n  points: TimeSeriesPoint[],\n  granularity: AnalyticsGranularity,\n): TimeSeriesPoint[] {\n  if (granularity === 'day') return points;\n\n  const buckets = new Map<string, TimeSeriesPoint>();\n  for (const point of points) {\n    const date = new Date(point.date);\n    if (isNaN(date.getTime())) continue;\n\n    if (granularity === 'week') {\n      const day = date.getUTCDay() || 7;\n      date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() - day + 1);\n    } else {\n      date.setUTCDate(1);\n    }\n\n    const bucketDate = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n    buckets.set(bucketDate, { date: bucketDate, value: point.value });\n  }\n\n  return Array.from(buckets.values()).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Controller\n// ============================================================================\n\n@ApiTags('Analytics')\n@Controller('analytics')\nexport class AnalyticsController {\n  constructor(private readonly analyticsService: AnalyticsService) {}\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Dashboard Summary\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('dashboard')\n  getDashboardSummary(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getDashboardSummary']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getDashboardSummary();\n  }\n\n  @Get('kpi-comparisons')\n  getKpiComparisons(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getKpiComparisons']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getKpiComparisons();\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Tenant Metrics\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('tenants')\n  getTenantMetrics(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getTenantMetrics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getTenantMetrics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('tenants/growth')\n  async getTenantGrowthTrend(\n    @Query('range') range?: string,\n    @Query('granularity') granularity?: string,\n    @Query('period') period = 'month',\n    @Query('dataPoints') dataPoints: unknown = 12,\n  ): Promise<AnalyticsTrendResponse> {\n    if (range) {\n      const parsedRange = parseRangeParameter(range, granularity);\n      const trend = await this.analyticsService.getTenantGrowthTrend({\n        period: parsedRange.period,\n        dataPoints: parsedRange.dataPoints,\n      });\n      return toTimeSeriesResponse(trend, parsedRange.range, parsedRange.granularity, 'admin.analytics_snapshots');\n    }\n\n    // INPUT VALIDATION: Parse period (supports shorthand like '30d', '12m')\n    // If dataPoints is provided explicitly, use it; otherwise extract from period shorthand\n    const parsedPeriod = parsePeriodParameter(period, 12);\n\n    // If explicit dataPoints provided, validate and use it; otherwise use parsed value\n    const explicitDataPoints = dataPoints !== 12 ? validateDataPoints(dataPoints) : parsedPeriod.dataPoints;\n\n    return this.analyticsService.getTenantGrowthTrend({\n      period: parsedPeriod.period,\n      dataPoints: explicitDataPoints,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('tenants/churn')\n  getChurnRateTrend(\n    @Query('period') period = 'month',\n    @Query('dataPoints') dataPoints: unknown = 12,\n  ): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getChurnRateTrend']> {\n    // INPUT VALIDATION: Parse period (supports shorthand like '30d', '12m')\n    const parsedPeriod = parsePeriodParameter(period, 12);\n    const explicitDataPoints = dataPoints !== 12 ? validateDataPoints(dataPoints) : parsedPeriod.dataPoints;\n\n    return this.analyticsService.getChurnRateTrend({\n      period: parsedPeriod.period,\n      dataPoints: explicitDataPoints,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // User Metrics\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('users')\n  getUserMetrics(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getUserMetrics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getUserMetrics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('users/activity')\n  async getUserActivityTrend(\n    @Query('range') range?: string,\n    @Query('granularity') granularity?: string,\n    @Query('period') period = 'day',\n    @Query('dataPoints') dataPoints: unknown = 30,\n  ): Promise<AnalyticsTrendResponse> {\n    if (range) {\n      const parsedRange = parseRangeParameter(range, granularity);\n      const trend = await this.analyticsService.getUserActivityTrend({\n        period: parsedRange.period,\n        dataPoints: parsedRange.dataPoints,\n      });\n      return toTimeSeriesResponse(trend, parsedRange.range, parsedRange.granularity, 'admin.analytics_snapshots');\n    }\n\n    // INPUT VALIDATION: Parse period (supports shorthand like '30d', '12m')\n    const parsedPeriod = parsePeriodParameter(period, 30);\n    const explicitDataPoints = dataPoints !== 30 ? validateDataPoints(dataPoints) : parsedPeriod.dataPoints;\n\n    return this.analyticsService.getUserActivityTrend({\n      period: parsedPeriod.period,\n      dataPoints: explicitDataPoints,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('users/heatmap')\n  getUserActivityHeatmap(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getUserActivityHeatmap']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getUserActivityHeatmap();\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Financial Metrics\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('financial')\n  getFinancialMetrics(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getFinancialMetrics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getFinancialMetrics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('financial/revenue')\n  getRevenueTrend(\n    @Query('period') period = 'month',\n    @Query('dataPoints') dataPoints: unknown = 12,\n  ): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getRevenueTrend']> {\n    // INPUT VALIDATION: Parse period (supports shorthand like '30d', '12m')\n    const parsedPeriod = parsePeriodParameter(period, 12);\n    const explicitDataPoints = dataPoints !== 12 ? validateDataPoints(dataPoints) : parsedPeriod.dataPoints;\n\n    return this.analyticsService.getRevenueTrend({\n      period: parsedPeriod.period,\n      dataPoints: explicitDataPoints,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('financial/by-plan')\n  getRevenueByPlan(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getRevenueByPlanChart']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getRevenueByPlanChart();\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Revenue Analytics (Frontend API compatibility)\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Get revenue analytics - matches frontend RevenueAnalytics interface\n   */\n  @Get('revenue')\n  getRevenueAnalytics(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getRevenueAnalytics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getRevenueAnalytics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('revenue/by-plan')\n  getRevenueAnalyticsByPlan(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getRevenueByPlanAnalytics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getRevenueByPlanAnalytics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('revenue/trend')\n  async getRevenueAnalyticsTrend(\n    @Query('range') range?: string,\n    @Query('granularity') granularity?: string,\n    @Query('period') period = '12m',\n  ): Promise<TimeSeriesResponse | RevenueTrendAnalyticsResponse> {\n    if (range) {\n      const parsedRange = parseRangeParameter(range, granularity);\n      const trend = await this.analyticsService.getRevenueTrend({\n        period: parsedRange.period,\n        dataPoints: parsedRange.dataPoints,\n      });\n      return toTimeSeriesResponse(trend, parsedRange.range, parsedRange.granularity, 'admin.analytics_snapshots');\n    }\n\n    return this.analyticsService.getRevenueTrendAnalytics(period);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // System Metrics\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('system')\n  getSystemMetrics(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getSystemMetrics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getSystemMetrics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('system/api-calls')\n  getApiCallsTrend(\n    @Query('period') period: 'day' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year' = 'day',\n    @Query('dataPoints') dataPoints = 30,\n  ): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getApiCallsTrend']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getApiCallsTrend({ period, dataPoints });\n  }\n\n  @Get('system/errors')\n  getErrorRateTrend(\n    @Query('period') period: 'day' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year' = 'day',\n    @Query('dataPoints') dataPoints = 30,\n  ): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getErrorRateTrend']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getErrorRateTrend({ period, dataPoints });\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Usage Metrics\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('usage')\n  getUsageMetrics(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getUsageMetrics']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getUsageMetrics();\n  }\n\n  @Get('usage/modules')\n  getModuleUsageChart(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getModuleUsageChart']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getModuleUsageChart();\n  }\n\n  @Get('usage/features')\n  getFeatureAdoptionChart(): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getFeatureAdoptionChart']> {\n    return this.analyticsService.getFeatureAdoptionChart();\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Snapshots\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('snapshots')\n  async getSnapshots(\n    @Query('category') category: 'tenant' | 'user' | 'financial' | 'system' | 'usage',\n    @Query('startDate') startDate: string,\n    @Query('endDate') endDate: string,\n    @Query('snapshotType') snapshotType?: 'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly' | 'yearly',\n  ): ReturnType<AnalyticsService['getSnapshots']> {\n    // BUG-023 fix: validate date strings before constructing Date objects.\n    // new Date('invalid') silently produces Invalid Date which causes DB query errors.\n    const parsedStart = new Date(startDate);\n    const parsedEnd = new Date(endDate);\n\n    if (!startDate || isNaN(parsedStart.getTime())) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('startDate must be a valid ISO 8601 date string');\n    }\n    if (!endDate || isNaN(parsedEnd.getTime())) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('endDate must be a valid ISO 8601 date string');\n    }\n\n    return this.analyticsService.getSnapshots(\n      category,\n      {\n        startDate: parsedStart,\n        endDate: parsedEnd,\n      },\n      snapshotType,\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":423,"startLine":1,"totalLines":423}}}

from './dto/reports.dto';\nimport { Destructive, RequiresCapability } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\nimport { AuditedOperation } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/audit';\nimport {\n '@nestjs/swagger';\nimport { IsIn, IsString, IsOptional, IsBoolean, IsObject, IsArray } from 'class-validator';\nimport { Request, Response } from 'express';\n\nimport {\n  ReportType,\n  ReportFormat,\n  ReportRequest,\n  ReportResult,\n  ReportDefinition,\n  ReportExecution,\n  ReportDefinitionStatus,\n  ReportSchedule,\n  ReportExecutionStatus,\n} from '../entities/analytics-snapshot.entity';\nimport { ReportsService } from '../services/reports.service';\nimport type { PaginationResultV1 } from '@platform/pagination-contracts';\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Controller\n// ============================================================================\n\n@ApiTags('Analytics')\n@Controller('reports')\nexport class ReportsController {\n  constructor(private readonly reportsService: ReportsService) {}\n\n  /** H-1 fix: Sanitize filenames to prevent Content-Disposition header injection */\n  private sanitizeFilename(filename: string): string {\n    // Strip path separators, control characters, quotes, and newlines\n    return filename\n      .replace(/[/\\\\:*?\"<>|\\r\\n]/g, '_')\n      .replace(/\\.{2,}/g, '.')\n      .slice(0, 255);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Report Definitions (Saved Reports)\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('definitions')\n  async getDefinitions(\n    @Query('status') status?: ReportDefinitionStatus,\n    @Query('type') type?: ReportType,\n    @Query('page') page?: number,\n    @Query('limit') limit?: number,\n  ): Promise<PaginationResultV1<ReportDefinition>> {\n    return this.reportsService.getDefinitions({\n      status,\n      type,\n      page: page ? Number(page) : undefined,\n      limit: limit ? Number(limit) : undefined,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('definitions/:id')\n  async getDefinition(@Param('id') id: string): Promise<ReportDefinition> {\n    return this.reportsService.getDefinition(id);\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Definition', action: 'CREATE' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('definitions')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)\n  async createDefinition(@Body() dto: CreateDefinitionDto): Promise<ReportDefinition> {\n    return this.reportsService.createDefinition(dto);\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Definition', action: 'UPDATE' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Put('definitions/:id')\n  async updateDefinition(\n    @Param('id') id: string,\n    @Body() dto: UpdateDefinitionDto,\n  ): Promise<ReportDefinition> {\n    return this.reportsService.updateDefinition(id, dto);\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Definition', action: 'DELETE' })\n  @Destructive()\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Delete('definitions/:id')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)\n  async deleteDefinition(@Param('id') id: string): Promise<void> {\n    return this.reportsService.deleteDefinition(id);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Report Executions (Execution History)\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('executions')\n  async getExecutions(\n    @Query('definitionId') definitionId?: string,\n    @Query('status') status?: ReportExecutionStatus,\n    @Query('reportType') reportType?: ReportType,\n    @Query('page') page?: number,\n    @Query('limit') limit?: number,\n  ): Promise<PaginationResultV1<ReportExecution>> {\n    return this.reportsService.getExecutions({\n      definitionId,\n      status,\n      reportType,\n      page: page ? Number(page) : undefined,\n      limit: limit ? Number(limit) : undefined,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Execution', action: 'CREATE' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('executions')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)\n  async createExecution(\n    @Body() dto: ExecuteReportDto,\n    @Req() req: Request & { user?: { id?: string; email?: string } },\n  ): Promise<ReportExecution> {\n    const startDate = dto.startDate ? new Date(dto.startDate) : undefined;\n    const endDate = dto.endDate ? new Date(dto.endDate) : undefined;\n\n    if (dto.startDate && (!startDate || isNaN(startDate.getTime()))) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid startDate format');\n    }\n\n    if (dto.endDate && (!endDate || isNaN(endDate.getTime()))) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid endDate format');\n    }\n\n    if (startDate && endDate && startDate > endDate) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Start date must be before end date');\n    }\n\n    return this.reportsService.executeReport({\n      definitionId: dto.definitionId ?? dto.reportId,\n      reportType: dto.reportType,\n      reportName: dto.reportName,\n      format: dto.format,\n      filters: dto.filters,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n      executedBy: req.user?.id,\n      executedByEmail: req.user?.email,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('executions/:id')\n  async getExecution(@Param('id') id: string): Promise<ReportExecution> {\n    return this.reportsService.getExecution(id);\n  }\n\n  @Get('executions/:id/download')\n  async downloadExecution(@Param('id') id: string, @Res() res: Response): Promise<void> {\n    const download = await this.reportsService.getExecutionDownload(id);\n\n    res.setHeader('Content-Type', download.contentType);\n    // H-1 fix: sanitize filename to prevent Content-Disposition header injection\n    const safeFilename = this.sanitizeFilename(download.filename);\n    res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${safeFilename}\"`);\n\n    res.send(download.data);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Quick Reports (Frontend Compatible)\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Reports', action: 'QUICK_TENANTS_REPORT' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('quick/tenants')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)\n  async quickTenantsReport(@Body() dto: QuickReportDto): Promise<ReportExecution> {\n    return this.reportsService.generateQuickTenantsReport(dto.format, dto.filters);\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Reports', action: 'QUICK_USERS_REPORT' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('quick/users')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)\n  async quickUsersReport(@Body() dto: QuickReportDto): Promise<ReportExecution> {\n    return this.reportsService.generateQuickUsersReport(dto.format, dto.filters);\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Reports', action: 'QUICK_REVENUE_REPORT' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('quick/revenue')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)\n  async quickRevenueReport(@Body() dto: QuickReportDto): Promise<ReportExecution> {\n    return this.reportsService.generateQuickRevenueReport(dto.format, dto.filters);\n  }\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Reports', action: 'QUICK_AUDIT_REPORT' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('quick/audit')\n  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)\n  async quickAuditReport(@Body() dto: QuickReportDto): Promise<ReportExecution> {\n    return this.reportsService.generateQuickAuditReport(dto.format, dto.filters);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Report Types\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('types')\n  getAvailableReports(): ReturnType<ReportsService['getAvailableReports']> {\n    return this.reportsService.getAvailableReports();\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Report Generation\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @AuditedOperation({ resource: 'Report', action: 'GENERATE' })\n  @RequiresCapability('security-ops')\n  @Post('generate')\n  async generateReport(@Body() dto: GenerateReportDto): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    // Validate dates\n    const startDate = new Date(dto.startDate);\n    const endDate = new Date(dto.endDate);\n\n    if (isNaN(startDate.getTime()) || isNaN(endDate.getTime())) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid date format');\n    }\n\n    if (startDate > endDate) {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Start date must be before end date');\n    }\n\n    const request: ReportRequest = {\n      type: dto.type,\n      format: dto.format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n      filters: dto.filters,\n      includeCharts: dto.includeCharts,\n    };\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport(request);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Quick Reports\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('tenant-overview')\n  async getTenantOverviewReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - 1);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'tenant_overview',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('churn-analysis')\n  async getChurnAnalysisReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n    @Query('months') months = 3,\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - months);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'tenant_churn',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('revenue')\n  async getRevenueReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n    @Query('days') days = 30,\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - days);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'financial_revenue',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('payments')\n  async getPaymentsReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - 1);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'financial_payments',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('module-usage')\n  async getModuleUsageReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - 1);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'usage_modules',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('feature-usage')\n  async getFeatureUsageReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - 1);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'usage_features',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  @Get('system-performance')\n  async getSystemPerformanceReport(\n    @Query('format') format: ReportFormat = 'json',\n    @Query('days') days = 7,\n  ): Promise<ReportResult> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - days);\n\n    return this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: 'system_performance',\n      format,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Download\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('download/:reportType')\n  async downloadReport(\n    @Param('reportType') reportType: ReportType,\n    @Query('format') format: 'pdf' | 'csv' = 'pdf',\n    @Query('days') days = 30,\n    @Res() res: Response,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    // MED-007 fix: validate reportType against the known enum at runtime\n    // (TypeScript types are erased at runtime — an invalid value would reach generateReport()\n    // and the Content-Disposition header if the switch default did not throw).\n    const allowedReportTypes: readonly ReportType[] = [\n      'tenant_overview', 'tenant_churn', 'financial_revenue',\n      'financial_payments', 'usage_modules', 'usage_features', 'system_performance',\n    ];\n    if (!allowedReportTypes.includes(reportType)) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid report type: \"${reportType}\"`);\n    }\n\n    // MED-007 fix: validate format parameter\n    const allowedFormats: ReadonlyArray<'pdf' | 'csv'> = ['pdf', 'csv'];\n    if (!allowedFormats.includes(format)) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid format: \"${format}\". Must be \"pdf\" or \"csv\"`);\n    }\n\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - days);\n\n    // Generate the report data\n    const report = await this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: reportType,\n      format: 'json',\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n\n    if (format === 'pdf') {\n      const pdfBuffer = await this.reportsService.generatePdfBuffer(reportType, report.data);\n      const filename = `${reportType}_report_${Date.now()}.pdf`;\n\n      res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');\n      res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${this.sanitizeFilename(filename)}\"`);\n      res.setHeader('Content-Length', pdfBuffer.length);\n      res.send(pdfBuffer);\n    } else {\n      // CSV format\n      const csvReport = await this.reportsService.generateReport({\n        type: reportType,\n        format: 'csv',\n        startDate,\n        endDate,\n      });\n      const filename = `${reportType}_report_${Date.now()}.csv`;\n\n      res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/csv');\n      res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${this.sanitizeFilename(filename)}\"`);\n      res.send(csvReport.data);\n    }\n  }\n\n  @Get('export/pdf/:reportType')\n  async exportPdf(\n    @Param('reportType') reportType: ReportType,\n    @Query('days') days = 30,\n    @Res() res: Response,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - days);\n\n    const report = await this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: reportType,\n      format: 'json',\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n\n    const pdfBuffer = await this.reportsService.generatePdfBuffer(reportType, report.data);\n    const filename = `${reportType}_report_${Date.now()}.pdf`;\n\n    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');\n    // BUG-040 fix: quote the filename per RFC 6266 to handle special characters safely\n    res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${this.sanitizeFilename(filename)}\"`);\n    res.setHeader('Content-Length', pdfBuffer.length);\n    res.send(pdfBuffer);\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Export Data\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  @Get('export/csv')\n  async exportCsv(\n    @Query('type') type: string,\n    @Res() res: Response,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const endDate = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date();\n    startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - 1);\n\n    let reportType: ReportType;\n    switch (type) {\n      case 'tenants':\n        reportType = 'tenant_overview';\n        break;\n      case 'users':\n        reportType = 'usage_modules';\n        break;\n      case 'revenue':\n        reportType = 'financial_revenue';\n        break;\n      case 'payments':\n        reportType = 'financial_payments';\n        break;\n      default:\n        throw new BadRequestException('Invalid export type');\n    }\n\n    const report = await this.reportsService.generateReport({\n      type: reportType,\n      format: 'csv',\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n    });\n\n    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/csv');\n    const csvFilename = this.sanitizeFilename(`${type}_report_${Date.now()}.csv`);\n    res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"${csvFilename}\"`);\n    res.send(report.data);\n  }\n}\n","numLines":523,"startLine":1,"totalLines":523}}}

AnalyticsSnapshot,\n10\t  ReportDefinition,\n11\t  ReportExecution,\n12\t} from '../../entities/analytics-snapshot.entity';\n13\timport { TenantReadOnly, TenantStatus, TenantPlan } from '../../entities/external/tenant.entity';\n14\timport { UserReadOnly } from '../../entities/external/user.entity';\n15\timport { AnalyticsService } from '../../services/analytics.service';\n16\timport { ReportsService } from '../../services/reports.service';\n17\t\n18\t// =============================================================================\n19\t// Mock Factories\n20\t// =============================================================================\n21\t\n22\tconst createMockRedisService = (): jest.Mocked<Partial<RedisService>> => ({\n23\t  getJson: jest.fn(),\n24\t  setJson: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),\n25\t  del: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(1),\n26\t  deletePattern: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(0),\n27\t});\n28\t\n29\tconst createMockTenant = (overrides: Partial<TenantReadOnly> = {}): TenantReadOnly =>\n30\t  ({\n31\t    id: 'tenant-1',\n32\t    name: 'Test Tenant',\n33\t    slug: 'test-tenant',\n34\t    status: TenantStatus.ACTIVE,\n35\t    plan: TenantPlan.STARTER,\n36\t    maxUsers: 10,\n37\t    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n38\t    updatedAt: new Date('2024-06-01'),\n39\t    ...overrides,\n40\t  }) as TenantReadOnly;\n41\t\n42\tconst mockAnalyticsService = {\n43\t  getTenantMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n44\t    totalTenants: 50,\n45\t    activeTenants: 30,\n46\t    churnRate: 4.2,\n47\t    avgRevenuePerTenant: 250,\n48\t  }),\n49\t  getFinancialMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n50\t    mrr: 12500,\n51\t    arr: 150000,\n52\t    totalRevenue: 145000,\n53\t    avgRevenuePerTenant: 250,\n54\t  }),\n55\t  getUsageMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n56\t    moduleUsage: {\n57\t      farm_management: { activeUsers: 100, totalSessions: 500, avgSessionDuration: 30 },\n58\t      sensor_monitoring: { activeUsers: 80, totalSessions: 400, avgSessionDuration: 25 },\n59\t    },\n60\t    featureAdoption: {\n61\t      batch_tracking: 72,\n62\t      water_quality: 65,\n63\t    },\n64\t  }),\n65\t  getSystemMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n66\t    uptimePercent: 99.95,\n67\t    avgResponseTime: 150,\n68\t    errorRate: 0.02,\n69\t  }),\n70\t};\n71\t\n72\tconst mockAuditLogService = {\n73\t  getStatistics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n74\t    totalLogs: 100,\n75\t    actionCounts: {},\n76\t  }),\n77\t};\n78\t\n79\tconst createMockDataSource = (): Partial<DataSource> => ({\n80\t  query: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),\n81\t});\n82\t\n83\tconst redisMock = <T extends (...args: never[]) => unknown>(\n84\t  fn: T | undefined,\n85\t): jest.MockedFunction<T> => {\n86\t  if (!fn) throw new Error('Redis mock method is missing');\n87\t  return fn as jest.MockedFunction<T>;\n88\t};\n89\t\n90\t// =============================================================================\n91\t// Tests\n92\t// =============================================================================\n93\t\n94\tdescribe('ReportsService - Caching', () => {\n95\t  let service: ReportsService;\n96\t  let mockRedis: jest.Mocked<Partial<RedisService>>;\n97\t  let mockTenantRepo: Record<string, jest.Mock>;\n98\t  let mockUserRepo: Record<string, jest.Mock>;\n99\t\n100\t  beforeEach(async () => {\n101\t    mockRedis = createMockRedisService();\n102\t    const mockDataSource = createMockDataSource();\n103\t\n104\t    mockTenantRepo = {\n105\t      find: jest\n106\t        .fn()\n107\t        .mockResolvedValue([\n108\t          createMockTenant(),\n109\t          createMockTenant({ id: 'tenant-2', name: 'Tenant 2', status: TenantStatus.ACTIVE }),\n110\t        ]),\n111\t      createQueryBuilder: jest.fn().mockReturnValue({\n112\t        select: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n113\t        addSelect: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n114\t        where: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n115\t        groupBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n116\t        getRawMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),\n117\t      }),\n118\t    };\n119\t\n120\t    mockUserRepo = {\n121\t      createQueryBuilder: jest.fn().mockReturnValue({\n122\t        select: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n123\t        addSelect: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n124\t        where: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n125\t        groupBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n126\t        getRawMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),\n127\t      }),\n128\t    };\n129\t\n130\t    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n131\t      providers: [\n132\t        ReportsService,\n133\t        { provide: getRepositoryToken(AnalyticsSnapshot), useValue: { find: jest.fn() } },\n134\t        { provide: getRepositoryToken(TenantReadOnly), useValue: mockTenantRepo },\n135\t        { provide: getRepositoryToken(UserReadOnly), useValue: mockUserRepo },\n136\t        {\n137\t          provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportDefinition),\n138\t          useValue: {\n139\t            createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n140\t            create: jest.fn(),\n141\t            save: jest.fn(),\n142\t            findOne: jest.fn(),\n143\t            remove: jest.fn(),\n144\t          },\n145\t        },\n146\t        {\n147\t          provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportExecution),\n148\t          useValue: {\n149\t            createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n150\t            create: jest.fn(),\n151\t            save: jest.fn(),\n152\t            findOne: jest.fn(),\n153\t          },\n154\t        },\n155\t        { provide: AnalyticsService, useValue: mockAnalyticsService },\n156\t        { provide: AuditLogService, useValue: mockAuditLogService },\n157\t        { provide: DataSource, useValue: mockDataSource },\n158\t        { provide: RedisService, useValue: mockRedis },\n159\t      ],\n160\t    }).compile();\n161\t\n162\t    service = module.get<ReportsService>(ReportsService);\n163\t  });\n164\t\n165\t  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n166\t  // getCachedOrCompute Pattern\n167\t  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n168\t\n169\t  describe('report caching via getCachedOrCompute', () => {\n170\t    it('should cache tenant_overview report on first request', async () => {\n171\t      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n172\t\n173\t      const result = await service.generateReport({\n174\t        type: 'tenant_overview',\n175\t        format: 'json',\n176\t        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n177\t        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n178\t      });\n179\t\n180\t      expect(result.type).toBe('tenant_overview');\n181\t      expect(mockRedis.getJson).toHaveBeenCalled();\n182\t      // After cache miss, setJson should be called to cache the result\n183\t      expect(mockRedis.setJson).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n184\t        expect.stringContaining('report:tenant_overview:'),\n185\t        expect.any(Object),\n186\t        14400, // 4 hour TTL\n187\t      );\n188\t    });\n189\t\n190\t    it('should return cached result on second request', async () => {\n191\t      const cachedData = {\n192\t        data: [{ id: 'tenant-1', name: 'Cached Tenant' }],\n193\t        summary: { totalTenants: 1 },\n194\t      };\n195\t      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(cachedData);\n196\t\n197\t      const result = await service.generateReport({\n198\t        type: 'tenant_overview',\n199\t        format: 'json',\n200\t        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n201\t        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n202\t { RedisService } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/redis';\nimport { BadRequestException, InternalServerErrorException } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport { getRepositoryToken } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\nimport { DataSource } from 'typeorm';\n\nimport { AuditLogService } from '../../../audit/audit.service';\nimport {\n ReportsService } from '../../services/reports.service';\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Mock Factories\n// =============================================================================\n\nconst createMockRedisService = (): jest.Mocked<Partial<RedisService>> => ({\n  getJson: jest.fn(),\n  setJson: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),\n  del: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(1),\n  deletePattern: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(0),\n});\n\nconst createMockTenant = (overrides: Partial<TenantReadOnly> = {}): TenantReadOnly =>\n  ({\n    id: 'tenant-1',\n    name: 'Test Tenant',\n    slug: 'test-tenant',\n    status: TenantStatus.ACTIVE,\n    plan: TenantPlan.STARTER,\n    maxUsers: 10,\n    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n    updatedAt: new Date('2024-06-01'),\n    ...overrides,\n  }) as TenantReadOnly;\n\nconst mockAnalyticsService = {\n  getTenantMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n    totalTenants: 50,\n    activeTenants: 30,\n    churnRate: 4.2,\n    avgRevenuePerTenant: 250,\n  }),\n  getFinancialMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n    mrr: 12500,\n    arr: 150000,\n    totalRevenue: 145000,\n    avgRevenuePerTenant: 250,\n  }),\n  getUsageMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n    moduleUsage: {\n      farm_management: { activeUsers: 100, totalSessions: 500, avgSessionDuration: 30 },\n      sensor_monitoring: { activeUsers: 80, totalSessions: 400, avgSessionDuration: 25 },\n    },\n    featureAdoption: {\n      batch_tracking: 72,\n      water_quality: 65,\n    },\n  }),\n  getSystemMetrics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n    uptimePercent: 99.95,\n    avgResponseTime: 150,\n    errorRate: 0.02,\n  }),\n};\n\nconst mockAuditLogService = {\n  getStatistics: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n    totalLogs: 100,\n    actionCounts: {},\n  }),\n};\n\nconst createMockDataSource = (): Partial<DataSource> => ({\n  query: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),\n});\n\nconst redisMock = <T extends (...args: never[]) => unknown>(\n  fn: T | undefined,\n): jest.MockedFunction<T> => {\n  if (!fn) throw new Error('Redis mock method is missing');\n  return fn as jest.MockedFunction<T>;\n};\n\n// =============================================================================\n// Tests\n// =============================================================================\n\ndescribe('ReportsService - Caching', () => {\n  let service: ReportsService;\n  let mockRedis: jest.Mocked<Partial<RedisService>>;\n  let mockTenantRepo: Record<string, jest.Mock>;\n  let mockUserRepo: Record<string, jest.Mock>;\n\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    mockRedis = createMockRedisService();\n    const mockDataSource = createMockDataSource();\n\n    mockTenantRepo = {\n      find: jest\n        .fn()\n        .mockResolvedValue([\n          createMockTenant(),\n          createMockTenant({ id: 'tenant-2', name: 'Tenant 2', status: TenantStatus.ACTIVE }),\n        ]),\n      createQueryBuilder: jest.fn().mockReturnValue({\n        select: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        addSelect: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        where: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        groupBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        getRawMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),\n      }),\n    };\n\n    mockUserRepo = {\n      createQueryBuilder: jest.fn().mockReturnValue({\n        select: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        addSelect: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        where: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        groupBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        getRawMany: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),\n      }),\n    };\n\n    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      providers: [\n        ReportsService,\n        { provide: getRepositoryToken(AnalyticsSnapshot), useValue: { find: jest.fn() } },\n        { provide: getRepositoryToken(TenantReadOnly), useValue: mockTenantRepo },\n        { provide: getRepositoryToken(UserReadOnly), useValue: mockUserRepo },\n        {\n          provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportDefinition),\n          useValue: {\n            createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n            create: jest.fn(),\n            save: jest.fn(),\n            findOne: jest.fn(),\n            remove: jest.fn(),\n          },\n        },\n        {\n          provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportExecution),\n          useValue: {\n            createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n            create: jest.fn(),\n            save: jest.fn(),\n            findOne: jest.fn(),\n          },\n        },\n        { provide: AnalyticsService, useValue: mockAnalyticsService },\n        { provide: AuditLogService, useValue: mockAuditLogService },\n        { provide: DataSource, useValue: mockDataSource },\n        { provide: RedisService, useValue: mockRedis },\n      ],\n    }).compile();\n\n    service = module.get<ReportsService>(ReportsService);\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // getCachedOrCompute Pattern\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('report caching via getCachedOrCompute', () => {\n    it('should cache tenant_overview report on first request', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      const result = await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(result.type).toBe('tenant_overview');\n      expect(mockRedis.getJson).toHaveBeenCalled();\n      // After cache miss, setJson should be called to cache the result\n      expect(mockRedis.setJson).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n        expect.stringContaining('report:tenant_overview:'),\n        expect.any(Object),\n        14400, // 4 hour TTL\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('should return cached result on second request', async () => {\n      const cachedData = {\n        data: [{ id: 'tenant-1', name: 'Cached Tenant' }],\n        summary: { totalTenants: 1 },\n      };\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(cachedData);\n\n      const result = await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      // DB should NOT be hit when cache returns data\n      expect(mockTenantRepo['find']).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n      expect(result.title).toBe('Tenant Overview Report');\n    });\n\n    it('should use 4 hour (14400s) TTL for report cache', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_churn',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(mockRedis.setJson).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.any(String), expect.any(Object), 14400);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Cache Key Generation\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('cache key generation', () => {\n    it('should include report type in cache key', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(mockRedis.getJson).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n        expect.stringContaining('report:tenant_overview:'),\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('should include date range in cache key', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n      const startDate = new Date('2024-06-01');\n      const endDate = new Date('2024-06-30');\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'system_performance',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate,\n        endDate,\n      });\n\n      expect(mockRedis.getJson).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n        expect.stringContaining(startDate.toISOString()),\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('should generate different cache keys for different date ranges', async () => {\n      const getJsonMock = redisMock(mockRedis.getJson);\n      getJsonMock.mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-06-30'),\n      });\n\n      const firstKey = (getJsonMock.mock.calls as Array<[string]>)[0]?.[0];\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-07-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      const secondKey = (getJsonMock.mock.calls as Array<[string]>)[1]?.[0];\n\n      expect(firstKey).not.toEqual(secondKey);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Cache Error Resilience\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('cache error resilience', () => {\n    it('should fall back to computation when cache read fails', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockRejectedValue(new Error('Redis down'));\n\n      const result = await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(result.type).toBe('tenant_overview');\n      expect(mockTenantRepo['find']).toHaveBeenCalled();\n    });\n\n    it('should not throw when cache write fails after computation', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n      redisMock(mockRedis.setJson).mockRejectedValue(new Error('Redis write error'));\n\n      const result = await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(result.type).toBe('tenant_overview');\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Without Redis (Optional dependency)\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('without Redis service', () => {\n    it('should compute reports when RedisService is not available', async () => {\n      const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n        providers: [\n          ReportsService,\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(AnalyticsSnapshot), useValue: { find: jest.fn() } },\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(TenantReadOnly), useValue: mockTenantRepo },\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(UserReadOnly), useValue: mockUserRepo },\n          {\n            provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportDefinition),\n            useValue: {\n              createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n              create: jest.fn(),\n              save: jest.fn(),\n              findOne: jest.fn(),\n              remove: jest.fn(),\n            },\n          },\n          {\n            provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportExecution),\n            useValue: {\n              createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n              create: jest.fn(),\n              save: jest.fn(),\n              findOne: jest.fn(),\n            },\n          },\n          { provide: AnalyticsService, useValue: mockAnalyticsService },\n          { provide: AuditLogService, useValue: mockAuditLogService },\n          { provide: DataSource, useValue: createMockDataSource() },\n          // No RedisService provided\n        ],\n      }).compile();\n\n      const serviceWithoutRedis = module.get<ReportsService>(ReportsService);\n\n      const result = await serviceWithoutRedis.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(result.type).toBe('tenant_overview');\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Cached Report Types\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('cached vs uncached report types', () => {\n    it('should cache tenant_overview reports', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(mockRedis.setJson).toHaveBeenCalled();\n    });\n\n    it('should cache tenant_churn reports', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'tenant_churn',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(mockRedis.setJson).toHaveBeenCalled();\n    });\n\n    it('should cache system_performance reports', async () => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n\n      await service.generateReport({\n        type: 'system_performance',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      });\n\n      expect(mockRedis.setJson).toHaveBeenCalled();\n    });\n\n    it('should throw BadRequestException for unknown report type', async () => {\n      const invalidRequest = {\n        type: 'nonexistent_report',\n        format: 'json',\n        startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n        endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n      } as unknown as Parameters<ReportsService['generateReport']>[0];\n\n      await expect(service.generateReport(invalidRequest)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Report Definitions Pagination\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('report definitions pagination', () => {\n    let mockDefRepo: Record<string, jest.Mock>;\n    let mockDefQueryBuilder: Record<string, jest.Mock>;\n\n    beforeEach(async () => {\n      mockDefQueryBuilder = {\n        andWhere: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        orderBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        skip: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        take: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n        getManyAndCount: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([[], 0]),\n      };\n\n      mockDefRepo = {\n        createQueryBuilder: jest.fn().mockReturnValue(mockDefQueryBuilder),\n        create: jest.fn(),\n        save: jest.fn(),\n        findOne: jest.fn(),\n        remove: jest.fn(),\n      };\n\n      const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n        providers: [\n          ReportsService,\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(AnalyticsSnapshot), useValue: { find: jest.fn() } },\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(TenantReadOnly), useValue: mockTenantRepo },\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(UserReadOnly), useValue: mockUserRepo },\n          { provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportDefinition), useValue: mockDefRepo },\n          {\n            provide: getRepositoryToken(ReportExecution),\n            useValue: {\n              createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),\n              create: jest.fn(),\n              save: jest.fn(),\n              findOne: jest.fn(),\n            },\n          },\n          { provide: AnalyticsService, useValue: mockAnalyticsService },\n          { provide: AuditLogService, useValue: mockAuditLogService },\n          { provide: DataSource, useValue: createMockDataSource() },\n          { provide: RedisService, useValue: mockRedis },\n        ],\n      }).compile();\n\n      service = module.get<ReportsService>(ReportsService);\n    });\n\n    it('should default to page 1, limit 20', async () => {\n      await service.getDefinitions();\n\n      expect(mockDefQueryBuilder['skip']).toHaveBeenCalledWith(0);\n      expect(mockDefQueryBuilder['take']).toHaveBeenCalledWith(20);\n    });\n\n    it('should apply custom page and limit', async () => {\n      await service.getDefinitions({ page: 3, limit: 10 });\n\n      expect(mockDefQueryBuilder['skip']).toHaveBeenCalledWith(20); // (3-1)*10\n      expect(mockDefQueryBuilder['take']).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10);\n    });\n\n    it('should filter by status', async () => {\n      await service.getDefinitions({ status: 'active' });\n\n      expect(mockDefQueryBuilder['andWhere']).toHaveBeenCalledWith('def.status = :status', {\n        status: 'active',\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should filter by type', async () => {\n      await service.getDefinitions({ type: 'tenant_overview' });\n\n      expect(mockDefQueryBuilder['andWhere']).toHaveBeenCalledWith('def.type = :type', {\n        type: 'tenant_overview',\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should return correct pagination metadata', async () => {\n      mockDefQueryBuilder['getManyAndCount'] = jest\n        .fn()\n        .mockResolvedValue([[{ id: '1', name: 'Report 1' }], 15]);\n\n      const result = await service.getDefinitions({ page: 2, limit: 5 });\n\n      expect(result.items).toHaveLength(1);\n      expect(result.total).toBe(15);\n      expect(result.page).toBe(2);\n      expect(result.limit).toBe(5);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // A failed report is a failure, not a report of zero\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('report generation failure', () => {\n    const REQUEST = {\n      type: 'financial_revenue' as const,\n      format: 'json' as const,\n      startDate: new Date('2024-01-01'),\n      endDate: new Date('2024-12-31'),\n    };\n\n    beforeEach(() => {\n      redisMock(mockRedis.getJson).mockResolvedValue(null);\n      const findTenants = mockTenantRepo['find'];\n      if (!findTenants) {\n        throw new Error('tenant repository mock lost find(); the revenue report reads through it');\n      }\n      findTenants.mockRejectedValue(new Error('relation \"tenants\" does not exist'));\n    });\n\n    it('refuses instead of answering with a zeroed report', async () => {\n      // The old behaviour returned 200 with totalRevenue: 0 — indistinguishable\n      // from a real period that earned nothing.\n      await expect(service.generateReport(REQUEST)).rejects.toThrow(InternalServerErrorException);\n    });\n\n    it('keeps the upstream detail in the log, not in the response', async () => {\n      await expect(service.generateReport(REQUEST)).rejects.toThrow(\n        /Failed to generate the revenue report/,\n      );\n      await expect(service.generateReport(REQUEST)).rejects.not.toThrow(/relation \"tenants\"/);\n    });\n\n    it('never caches the failure', async () => {\n      // This report runs through getCachedOrCompute. A zeroed body used to be\n      // written to Redis and served for the whole 4-hour TTL, so one transient\n      // outage kept reporting zero revenue long after it ended.\n      await expect(service.generateReport(REQUEST)).rejects.toThrow();\n\n      expect(mockRedis.setJson).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n        expect.stringContaining('report:financial_revenue:'),\n        expect.anything(),\n        expect.anything(),\n      );\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":570,"startLine":1,"totalLines":570}}}

'financial' | 'system' | 'usage';\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Metric Interfaces\n// suspended: number;\n  newThisMonth: number;\n  churnedThisMonth: number;\n  churnRate: number;\n  growthRate: number;\n  byPlan: Record<string, number>;\n  byRegion: Record<string, number;\n  byPlan: Record<string, number>;\n  byCurrency: Record<string, number>;\n}\n\nexport interface SystemMetrics {\n  totalStorageBytes: number;\n  usedStorageBytes: number;\n queuedJobs: number;\n}\n\nexport interface UsageMetrics {\n  moduleUsage: Record<string, {\n    activeUsers: number;\n    totalSessions: number;\n    avgSessionDuration: number;\n  }>;\n  featureAdoption: Record<string, number>;\n  topFeatures: Array<{ feature: string; usage: number }>;\n  peakHours: number[];\n  avgDailyActiveUsers: number;\n}\n\n// all common query patterns\n// (filter by category + optional snapshotType, ordered by snapshotDate)\n@Index(['category', 'snapshotType', 'snapshotDate'])\nexport class AnalyticsSnapshot {\n MetricCategory;\n\n  @Column({ type: 'date' })\n  snapshotDate!: Date;\n\n  @Column({ type: 'jsonb' })\n  metrics!: TenantMetrics | UserMetrics | FinancialMetrics | SystemMetrics | UsageMetrics;\n\n  @Column({ type: 'jsonb', nullable: true })\n  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\n\n  @CreateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' })\n  createdAt!: Date;\n}\n\n// DashboardSummary {\n  tenants: TenantMetrics;\n  users: UserMetrics;\n  financial: FinancialMetrics;\n  system: SystemMetrics;\n  usage: UsageMetrics;\n  generatedAt: Date;\n  /**\n   * Lists which data sources failed during aggregation.\n   * Present only when the dashboard is in degraded mode (partial failure).\n   * Possible values: 'tenants', 'users', 'financial', 'system', 'usage'.\n   */\n  unavailable?: string[];\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Time Series Data\n// PlanPricing {\n  basePrice: number;\n  perFarmPrice?: number;\n  perSensorPrice?: number;\n  perUserPrice?: number;\n  currency: string;\n}\n\n// C-6 fix: billing-service stores subscriptions in the 'billing' schema, not 'public'\n// Column names explicitly mapped to snake_case as defined by billing-service schema\n@Entity('subscriptions', { schema: 'billing', synchronize: false })\nexport class SubscriptionReadOnly {\n  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')\n  id!: string;\n\n  @Column({ type: 'uuid', name: 'tenant_id' })\n  tenantId!: string;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'plan_tier', type: 'enum', enum: PlanTier })\n  planTier!: PlanTier;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'plan_name' })\n  planName!: string;\n\n  @Column({ type: 'enum', enum: SubscriptionStatus, default: SubscriptionStatus.TRIAL })\n  status!: SubscriptionStatus;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'billing_cycle', type: 'enum', enum: BillingCycle })\n  billingCycle!: BillingCycle;\n\n  @Column('jsonb')\n  pricing!: PlanPricing;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'start_date', type: 'timestamptz' })\n  startDate!: Date;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'end_date', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  endDate!: Date | null;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'current_period_start', type: 'timestamptz' })\n  currentPeriodStart!: Date;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'current_period_end', type: 'timestamptz' })\n  currentPeriodEnd!: Date;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'trial_end_date', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  trialEndDate!: Date | null;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'cancelled_at', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  cancelledAt!: Date | null;\n\n  @Column({ name: 'auto_renew', default: true })\n  autoRenew!: boolean;\n\n  @CreateDateColumn()\n  createdAt!: Date;\n\n  @UpdateDateColumn()\n  updatedAt!: Date;\n}\n","numLines":96,"startLine":1,"totalLines":96}}}
