{\n  INestApplication,\n  Controller,\n  Get,\n  Post,\n  Body,\n  Param,\n  HttpStatus,\n  HttpException,\n  NotFoundException,\n  ForbiddenException,\n  BadRequestException,\n  ConflictException,\n  InternalServerErrorException,\n  ValidationPipe,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { APP_FILTER } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport { IsString, IsNotEmpty, IsInt, Min } from 'class-validator';\nimport request from 'supertest';\n\nimport { Public } from '../../decorators/public.decorator';\nimport { GlobalExceptionFilter } from '../../filters/global-exception.filter';\n\nclass ValidatedDto {\n  @IsString()\n  @IsNotEmpty()\n  name!: string;\n\n  @IsInt()\n  @Min(1)\n  count!: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Controller that throws various error types so we can verify\n * the global exception filter returns a consistent error shape.\n */\n@Controller('error-test')\nclass ErrorTestController {\n  @Get('ok')\n  @Public()\n  ok() {\n    return { status: 'ok' };\n  }\n\n  @Get('bad-request')\n  @Public()\n  badRequest() {\n    throw new BadRequestException('Invalid input provided');\n  }\n\n  @Get('not-found')\n  @Public()\n  notFound() {\n    throw new NotFoundException('Resource not found');\n  }\n\n  @Get('forbidden')\n  @Public()\n  forbidden() {\n    throw new ForbiddenException('Access denied');\n  }\n\n  @Get('conflict')\n  @Public()\n  conflict() {\n    throw new ConflictException('Duplicate resource');\n  }\n\n  @Get('internal')\n  @Public()\n  internal() {\n    throw new InternalServerErrorException('Something went wrong');\n  }\n\n  @Get('generic-error')\n  @Public()\n  genericError() {\n    throw new Error('Unexpected failure');\n  }\n\n  @Get('custom-http')\n  @Public()\n  customHttp() {\n    throw new HttpException(\n      { message: 'Custom error', details: { field: 'value' } },\n      HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Post('validate')\n  @Public()\n  validate(@Body() dto: ValidatedDto) {\n    return dto;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Validates that the GlobalExceptionFilter produces a consistent\n * error response shape for all HTTP status codes.\n */\ndescribe('Error Response Format Consistency', () => {\n  let app: INestApplication;\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    const moduleFixture: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      controllers: [ErrorTestController],\n      providers: [\n        {\n          provide: APP_FILTER,\n          useClass: GlobalExceptionFilter,\n        },\n      ],\n    }).compile();\n\n    app = moduleFixture.createNestApplication();\n    app.useGlobalPipes(\n      new ValidationPipe({\n        whitelist: true,\n        forbidNonWhitelisted: true,\n        transform: true,\n        transformOptions: { enableImplicitConversion: false },\n        stopAtFirstError: false,\n      }),\n    );\n    await app.init();\n  });\n\n  afterAll(async () => {\n    await app.close();\n  });\n\n  /**\n   * Helper: asserts that every error response contains the expected\n   * standard fields: statusCode, message, error, timestamp, path.\n   */\n  function expectStandardErrorShape(\n    body: Record<string, unknown>,\n    expectedStatus: number,\n  ) {\n    expect(body).toHaveProperty('statusCode', expectedStatus);\n    expect(body).toHaveProperty('message');\n    expect(typeof body['message']).toBe('string');\n    expect(body).toHaveProperty('error');\n    expect(typeof body['error']).toBe('string');\n    expect(body).toHaveProperty('timestamp');\n    expect(typeof body['timestamp']).toBe('string');\n    // Timestamp should be ISO 8601\n    expect(new Date(body['timestamp'] as string).toISOString()).toBe(body['timestamp']);\n    expect(body).toHaveProperty('path');\n    expect(typeof body['path']).toBe('string');\n  }\n\n  describe('Standard HTTP exception responses', () => {\n    it('should return standard shape for 400 Bad Request', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/bad-request');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 400);\n      expect(response.body.error).toBe('Bad Request');\n    });\n\n    it('should return standard shape for 404 Not Found', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/not-found');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 404);\n      expect(response.body.error).toBe('Not Found');\n    });\n\n    it('should return standard shape for 403 Forbidden', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/forbidden');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 403);\n      expect(response.body.error).toBe('Forbidden');\n    });\n\n    it('should return standard shape for 409 Conflict', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/conflict');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.CONFLICT);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 409);\n      expect(response.body.error).toBe('Conflict');\n    });\n\n    it('should return standard shape for 500 Internal Server Error', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/internal');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 500);\n      expect(response.body.error).toBe('Internal Server Error');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('Non-HTTP exceptions (generic Error)', () => {\n    it('should return 500 with standard shape for unhandled Error', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/generic-error');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 500);\n      expect(response.body.error).toBe('Internal Server Error');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('Custom HTTP exception with details', () => {\n    it('should include details field when provided', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/custom-http');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY);\n      expectStandardErrorShape(response.body, 422);\n      expect(response.body.details).toEqual({ field: 'value' });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('Request ID forwarding', () => {\n    it('should include requestId when x-request-id header is sent', async () => {\n      const requestId = 'test-req-id-12345';\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/bad-request')\n        .set('x-request-id', requestId);\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n      expect(response.body.requestId).toBe(requestId);\n    });\n\n    it('should omit requestId when no x-request-id header', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/error-test/bad-request');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n      // requestId should be undefined (not present) or explicitly undefined\n      expect(response.body.requestId).toBeUndefined();\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('404 for non-existent routes', () => {\n    it('should return 404 for a completely unknown path', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/this-route-does-not-exist');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('Validation pipe error format', () => {\n    it('should return 400 for invalid body with standard error shape', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .post('/error-test/validate')\n        .send({ name: '', count: -1 });\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n      // The ValidationPipe throws HttpException which our filter catches\n      expect(response.body).toHaveProperty('statusCode', 400);\n      expect(response.body).toHaveProperty('message');\n      expect(response.body).toHaveProperty('timestamp');\n      expect(response.body).toHaveProperty('path');\n    });\n\n    it('should return 400 when required fields are missing', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .post('/error-test/validate')\n        .send({});\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n      expect(response.body.statusCode).toBe(400);\n    });\n\n    it('should return 400 for non-whitelisted properties', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .post('/error-test/validate')\n        .send({ name: 'valid', count: 5, extraField: 'not allowed' });\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('Error name mapping', () => {\n    const errorNameCases: [number, string][] = [\n      [400, 'Bad Request'],\n      [403, 'Forbidden'],\n      [404, 'Not Found'],\n      [409, 'Conflict'],\n      [422, 'Unprocessable Entity'],\n      [500, 'Internal Server Error'],\n    ];\n\n    it.each(errorNameCases)(\n      'status %i should map to error name \"%s\"',\n      async (expectedStatus, expectedName) => {\n        // We already tested each individually; this ensures the mapping table is consistent\n        const routeMap: Record<number, string> = {\n          400: '/error-test/bad-request',\n          403: '/error-test/forbidden',\n          404: '/error-test/not-found',\n          409: '/error-test/conflict',\n          422: '/error-test/custom-http',\n          500: '/error-test/internal',\n        };\n\n        const route = routeMap[expectedStatus]!;\n        const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n          .get(route);\n\n        expect(response.status).toBe(expectedStatus);\n        expect(response.body.error).toBe(expectedName);\n      },\n    );\n  });\n});\n","numLines":319,"startLine":1,"totalLines":319}}}

{\n  INestApplication,\n  Controller,\n  Get,\n  Post,\n  Body,\n  HttpStatus,\n  ValidationPipe,\n  VersioningType,\n  VERSION_NEUTRAL,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { SwaggerModule, DocumentBuilder } from '@nestjs/swagger';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport { IsString, IsNotEmpty } from 'class-validator';\nimport request from 'supertest';\n\nimport { Public } from '../../decorators/public.decorator';\n\nclass TestDto {\n  @IsString()\n  @IsNotEmpty()\n  name!: string;\n}\n\n@Controller('swagger-test')\nclass SwaggerTestController {\n  @Get('items')\n  @Public()\n  getItems() {\n    return [{ id: 1, name: 'test' }];\n  }\n\n  @Post('items')\n  @Public()\n  createItem(@Body() dto: TestDto) {\n    return { id: 2, ...dto };\n  }\n}\n\ndescribe('Swagger / OpenAPI Documentation', () => {\n  let app: INestApplication;\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    const moduleFixture: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      controllers: [SwaggerTestController],\n    }).compile();\n\n    app = moduleFixture.createNestApplication();\n\n    // Mirror the versioning config from main.ts\n    app.enableVersioning({\n      type: VersioningType.URI,\n      defaultVersion: ['1', VERSION_NEUTRAL],\n    });\n\n    // Mirror the Swagger config from main.ts\n    const swaggerConfig = new DocumentBuilder()\n      .setTitle('Aquaculture Admin API')\n      .setDescription(\n        'Platform administration API for the Aquaculture SaaS platform',\n      )\n      .setVersion('1.0.0')\n      .addBearerAuth(\n        { type: 'http', scheme: 'bearer', bearerFormat: 'JWT' },\n        'JWT',\n      )\n      .addServer('/', 'Direct (dev)')\n      .addServer('/api', 'Via nginx gateway')\n      .build();\n\n    const document = SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, swaggerConfig);\n    SwaggerModule.setup('docs', app, document, {\n      swaggerOptions: {\n        persistAuthorization: true,\n        tagsSorter: 'alpha',\n        operationsSorter: 'alpha',\n      },\n    });\n\n    await app.init();\n  });\n\n  afterAll(async () => {\n    await app.close();\n  });\n\n  describe('Swagger UI availability', () => {\n    it('should serve the Swagger UI page at /docs', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/docs/');\n\n      // Swagger UI redirects or returns HTML\n      expect([HttpStatus.OK, HttpStatus.MOVED_PERMANENTLY, HttpStatus.FOUND]).toContain(\n        response.status,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('should serve the OpenAPI JSON spec at /docs-json', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/docs-json');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(response.body).toBeDefined();\n      expect(response.body.openapi).toBeDefined();\n      expect(response.body.info).toBeDefined();\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('OpenAPI spec structure', () => {\n    let spec: Record<string, any>;\n\n    beforeAll(async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/docs-json');\n      spec = response.body;\n    });\n\n    it('should have valid OpenAPI 3.x version', () => {\n      expect(spec['openapi']).toMatch(/^3\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$/);\n    });\n\n    it('should contain correct API title and version', () => {\n      expect(spec['info'].title).toBe('Aquaculture Admin API');\n      expect(spec['info'].version).toBe('1.0.0');\n    });\n\n    it('should contain API description', () => {\n      expect(spec['info'].description).toContain('Aquaculture');\n    });\n\n    it('should define Bearer JWT security scheme', () => {\n      const securitySchemes = spec['components']?.securitySchemes;\n      expect(securitySchemes).toBeDefined();\n      expect(securitySchemes.JWT).toBeDefined();\n      expect(securitySchemes.JWT.type).toBe('http');\n      expect(securitySchemes.JWT.scheme).toBe('bearer');\n      expect(securitySchemes.JWT.bearerFormat).toBe('JWT');\n    });\n\n    it('should define server entries', () => {\n      expect(spec['servers']).toBeDefined();\n      expect(spec['servers'].length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n      const urls = spec['servers'].map((s: any) => s.url);\n      expect(urls).toContain('/');\n    });\n\n    it('should have paths section with documented endpoints', () => {\n      expect(spec['paths']).toBeDefined();\n      const pathKeys = Object.keys(spec['paths']);\n      expect(pathKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    });\n\n    it('documented endpoints should reference valid HTTP methods', () => {\n      const validMethods = ['get', 'post', 'put', 'patch', 'delete', 'options', 'head'];\n      for (const [, methods] of Object.entries(spec['paths'])) {\n        for (const method of Object.keys(methods as object)) {\n          if (method.startsWith('x-')) continue; // OpenAPI extensions\n          expect(validMethods).toContain(method);\n        }\n      }\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":162,"startLine":1,"totalLines":162}}}

expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n65\t      expect(response.body).toEqual({ version: 'default', message: 'pong' });\n66\t    });\n67\t\n68\t    it('should respond at /v1/ prefixed path', async () => {\n69\t      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n70\t        .get('/v1/test-versioning/ping');\n71\t\n72\t      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n73\t expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n88\t      expect(response.body).toEqual({ version: '2', message: 'v2 only' });\n89\t    });\n90\t\n91\t    it('should NOT serve @Version(\"2\") endpoint {\n  INestApplication,\n  Controller,\n  Get,\n  HttpStatus,\n  ValidationPipe,\n  VersioningType,\n  VERSION_NEUTRAL,\n  Version,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport request from 'supertest';\n\nimport { Public } from '../../decorators/public.decorator';\n\n/**\n * Test controller that exposes versioned and unversioned routes.\n * Uses @Public() to bypass auth — we're testing the versioning layer, not guards.\n */\n@Controller('test-versioning')\nclass TestVersioningController {\n  @Get('ping')\n  @Public()\n  ping() {\n    return { version: 'default', message: 'pong' };\n  }\n\n  @Get('v2-only')\n  @Public()\n  @Version('2')\n  v2Only() {\n    return { version: '2', message: 'v2 only' };\n  }\n}\n\ndescribe('API Versioning', () => {\n  let app: INestApplication;\n\n  beforeAll(async () => {\n    const moduleFixture: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      controllers: [TestVersioningController],\n    }).compile();\n\n    app = moduleFixture.createNestApplication();\n\n    // Mirror the exact versioning config from main.ts\n    app.enableVersioning({\n      type: VersioningType.URI,\n      defaultVersion: ['1', VERSION_NEUTRAL],\n    });\n\n    await app.init();\n  });\n\n  afterAll(async () => {\n    await app.close();\n  });\n\n  describe('URI-based versioning configuration', () => {\n    it('should respond at unversioned path (VERSION_NEUTRAL backward compat)', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/test-versioning/ping');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(response.body).toEqual({ version: 'default', message: 'pong' });\n    });\n\n    it('should respond at /v1/ prefixed path', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/v1/test-versioning/ping');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(response.body).toEqual({ version: 'default', message: 'pong' });\n    });\n\n    it('should return 404 for unknown version prefix', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/v99/test-versioning/ping');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n    });\n\n    it('should serve @Version(\"2\") endpoint at /v2/ prefix only', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/v2/test-versioning/v2-only');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(response.body).toEqual({ version: '2', message: 'v2 only' });\n    });\n\n    it('should NOT serve @Version(\"2\") endpoint at /v1/ prefix', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/v1/test-versioning/v2-only');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n    });\n\n    it('should NOT serve @Version(\"2\") endpoint at unversioned path', async () => {\n      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer())\n        .get('/test-versioning/v2-only');\n\n      expect(response.status).toBe(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('Backward compatibility', () => {\n    it('both /v1/ and unversioned paths return identical response bodies', async () => {\n      const [unversioned, versioned] = await Promise.all([\n        request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/test-versioning/ping'),\n        request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/v1/test-versioning/ping'),\n      ]);\n\n      expect(unversioned.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(versioned.status).toBe(HttpStatus.OK);\n      expect(unversioned.body).toEqual(versioned.body);\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":119,"startLine":1,"totalLines":119}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GovVCziNA9nyTYwuGTpQUM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Frontend-Backend Contract Validation Test\n3\t *\n4\t * Sprint 4 Fix - Grup Y (H12/46)\n5\t * ---------------------------------\n6\t * Bu test, admin-panel frontend'inin cagirdigi API endpoint'leri ile\n7\t * admin-api-service backend'inin sundugu endpoint'lerin uyumunu dogrular.\n8\t *\n9\t * Problem: 3 FIELD_MISMATCH production'da 400/404 uretiyordu ama CI/CD yakalayamiyordu.\n10\t * Cozum: Statik analiz ile frontend URL pattern'lerini ve backend controller path'lerini\n11\t *         extract edip, eslesme kontrolu yapan otomatik test.\n12\t *\n13\t * Yaklasim:\n14\t * - Frontend: services/api/*.ts dosyalarindan apiFetch cagrilarindaki URL pattern'leri cikarilir\n15\t * - Backend: Controller dekoratorlerinden (@Get, @Post, vb.) endpoint path'leri cikarilir\n16\t * - Eslesme: Her frontend URL'in backend'de bir karsiligi olmali\n17\t *\n18\t * Bakim notu: Yeni endpoint eklendiginde veya mevcut path degistiginde bu test kirilir.\n19\t * Bu beklenen ve istenen davranistir -- kontrat degisiklikleri bilinmeli.\n20\t */\n21\t\n22\timport * as fs from 'fs';\n23\timport * as path from 'path';\n24\timport * as ts from 'typescript';\n25\t\n26\t// ============================================================================\n27\t// Types\n28\t// ============================================================================\n29\t\n30\tinterface FrontendEndpoint {\n31\t  url: string;\n32\t  method: string;\n33\t  source: string; // api dosya adi\n34\t  functionName: string;\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\tinterface BackendEndpoint {\n38\t  path: string;\n39\t  method: string;\n40\t  controller: string;\n41\t  handler: string;\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\t// ============================================================================\n45\t// Frontend URL Extraction\n46\t// ============================================================================\n47\t\n48\t/**\n49\t * Frontend API dosyalarindan apiFetch cagrilarindaki URL pattern'lerini cikarir.\n50\t * Template literal'ler normalize edilir: ${param} -> :param\n51\t */\n52\tfunction extractFrontendEndpoints(): FrontendEndpoint[] {\n53\t  const apiDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../../../web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api');\n54\t\n55\t  if (!fs.existsSync(apiDir)) {\n56\t    throw new Error(`Frontend API directory not found: ${apiDir}`);\n57\t  }\n58\t\n59\t  const endpoints: FrontendEndpoint[] = [];\n60\t  const apiFiles = fs.readdirSync(apiDir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.ts'));\n61\t\n62\t  for (const file of apiFiles) {\n63\t    const filePath = path.join(apiDir, file);\n64\t    const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');\n65\t    const source = file.replace('.ts', '');\n66\t    const sourceFile = ts.createSourceFile(\n67\t      filePath,\n68\t      content,\n69\t      ts.ScriptTarget.Latest,\n70\t      true,\n71\t      ts.ScriptKind.TS,\n72\t    );\n73\t\n74\t    const visit = (node: ts.Node): void => {\n75\t      if (\n76\t        ts.isCallExpression(node) &&\n77\t        ts.isIdentifier(node.expression) &&\n78\t        node.expression.text === 'apiFetch'\n79\t      ) {\n80\t        const urlArgument = node.arguments[0];\n81\t        const rawUrl = urlArgument ? renderStaticUrl(urlArgument) : null;\n82\t\n83\t        // HATEOAS links validated and returned by the backend are deliberately\n84\t        // dynamic. They are not a second declaration of a route, so there is\n85\t        // no static frontend route to compare for those calls.\n86\t        if (rawUrl !== null) {\n87\t          const url = normalizeUrl(rawUrl);\n88\t          const method = extractMethod(node.arguments[1]);\n89\t          const functionName = findContainingFunctionName(node);\n90\t\n91\t          if (\n92\t            url &&\n93\t            !endpoints.some(\n94\t              (endpoint) =>\n95\t                endpoint.url === url && endpoint.method === method && endpoint.source === source,\n96\t            )\n97\t          ) {\n98\t            endpoints.push({ url, method, source, functionName });\n99\t          }\n100\t        }\n101\t      }\n102\t\n103\t      ts.forEachChild(node, visit);\n104\t    };\n105\t\n106\t    visit(sourceFile);\n107\t  }\n108\t\n109\t  return endpoints;\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\tfunction renderStaticUrl(expression: ts.Expression): string | null {\n113\t  if (ts.isStringLiteral(expression) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(expression)) {\n114\t    return expression.text;\n115\t  }\n116\t\n117\t  if (!ts.isTemplateExpression(expression)) return null;\n118\t\n119\t  let rendered = expression.head.text;\n120\t  for (const span of expression.templateSpans) {\n121\t    if (expressionIntroducesQuery(span.expression)) {\n122\t      rendered += '?';\n123\t      break;\n124\t    }\n125\t\n126\t    // A template whose first segment is a runtime path (for example a\n127\t    // backend-provided status URL) is not a statically declared route.\n128\t    if (rendered === '' && ts.isIdentifier(span.expression)) return null;\n129\t\n130\t    rendered += ':param';\n131\t    rendered += span.literal.text;\n132\t  }\n133\t  return rendered;\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\tfunction expressionIntroducesQuery(expression: ts.Expression): boolean {\n137\t  let introducesQuery = false;\n138\t  const visit = (node: ts.Node): void => {\n139\t    if (ts.isTemplateExpression(node) && node.head.text.startsWith('?')) {\n140\t      introducesQuery = true;\n141\t      return;\n142\t    }\n143\t    if (\n144\t      (ts.isStringLiteral(node) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(node)) &&\n145\t      node.text.startsWith('?')\n146\t    ) {\n147\t      introducesQuery = true;\n148\t      return;\n149\t    }\n150\t    ts.forEachChild(node, visit);\n151\t  };\n152\t  visit(expression);\n153\t  return introducesQuery;\n154\t}\n155\t\n156\tfunction findContainingFunctionName(node: ts.Node): string {\n157\t  let current: ts.Node | undefined = node.parent;\n158\t  while (current) {\n159\t    if (ts.isPropertyAssignment(current) || ts.isMethodDeclaration(current)) {\n160\t      return propertyNameText(current.name);\n161\t    }\n162\t    if (ts.isVariableDeclaration(current) && ts.isIdentifier(current.name)) {\n163\t      return current.name.text;\n164\t    }\n165\t    current = current.parent;\n166\t  }\n167\t  return 'unknown';\n168\t}\n169\t\n170\tfunction propertyNameText(name: ts.PropertyName | undefined): string {\n171\t  if (!name) return 'unknown';\n172\t  if (ts.isIdentifier(name) || ts.isStringLiteral(name) || ts.isNumericLiteral(name)) {\n173\t    return name.text;\n174\t  }\n175\t  return name.getText();\n176\t}\n177\t\n178\t/**\n179\t * URL'i normalize eder:\n180\t * - Query string'leri (?...) kaldirir\n181\t * - Template literal parametrelerini (${...}) :param'a cevirir\n182\t * - Trailing slash kaldirir\n183\t * - encodeURIComponent(...) kaldır\n184\t */\n185\tfunction normalizeUrl(rawUrl: string): string {\n186\t  // Query string'i kaldir\n187\t  let url = rawUrl.split('?')[0]!;\n188\t\n189\t  // ${buildQueryString(...)} kaldir\n190\t  url = url.replace(/\\$\\{buildQueryString\\([^)]*\\)\\}/g, '');\n191\t\n192\t  // ${encodeURIComponent(...)} -> :param\n193\t  url = url.replace(/\\$\\{encodeURIComponent\\([^)]*\\)\\}/g, ':param');\n194\t\n195\t  // Template literal parametrelerini :param'a cevir\n196\t  // ${variable} veya ${variable.property} veya ${expression}\n197\t  url = url.replace(/\\$\\{[^}]+\\}/g, ':param');\n198\t\n199\t  // Trailing slash kaldir\n200\t  url = url.replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n201\t\n202\t  // Bos path segment'leri temizle\n203\t  url = url.replace(/\\/+/g, '/');\n204\t\n205\t  return url;\n206\t}\n207\t\n208\t/**\n209\t * apiFetch cagrisindaki HTTP method'u cikarir.\n210\t * Varsayilan: GET\n211\t */\n212\tfunction extractMethod(options: ts.Expression | undefined): string {\n213\t  if (!options || !ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(options)) return 'GET';\n214\t\n215\t  for (const property of options.properties) {\n216\t    if (\n217\t      ts.isPropertyAssignment(property) &&\n218\t      propertyNameText(property.name) === 'method' &&\n219\t      ts.isStringLiteral(property.initializer)\n220\t    ) {\n221\t      return property.initializer.text.toUpperCase();\n222\t    }\n223\t  }\n224\t  return 'GET';\n225\t}\n226\t\n227\t// ============================================================================\n228\t// Backend Endpoint Extraction\n229\t// ============================================================================\n230\t\n231\t/**\n232\t * Backend controller dosyalarindan decorator-tabanli endpoint path'lerini cikarir.\n233\t * @Controller('prefix') + @Get('sub') => /prefix/sub\n234\t */\n235\tfunction extractBackendEndpoints(): BackendEndpoint[] {\n236\t  const srcDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');\n237\t  const endpoints: BackendEndpoint[] = [];\n238\t\n239\t  // Controller dosyalarini bul\n240\t  const controllerFiles = findControllerFiles(srcDir);\n241\t\n242\t  for (const filePath of controllerFiles) {\n243\t    const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');\n244\t    const relativePath = path.relative(srcDir, filePath);\n245\t    const sourceFile = ts.createSourceFile(\n246\t      filePath,\n247\t      content,\n248\t      ts.ScriptTarget.Latest,\n249\t      true,\n250\t      ts.ScriptKind.TS,\n251\t    );\n252\t\n253\t    for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n254\t      if (!ts.isClassDeclaration(statement)) continue;\n255\t      const controller = findDecorator(statement, 'Controller');\n256\t      if (!controller) continue;\n257\t      const controllerPrefix = decoratorPath(controller);\n258\t\n259\t      for (const member of statement.members) {\n260\t        if (!ts.isMethodDeclaration(member)) continue;\n261\t        for (const httpMethod of ['Get', 'Post', 'Put', 'Patch', 'Delete'] as const) {\n262\t          const decorator = findDecorator(member, httpMethod);\n263\t          if (!decorator) continue;\n264\t          const subPath = decoratorPath(decorator);\n265\t          const fullPath = normalizeUrl(`/${controllerPrefix}/${subPath}`);\n266\t\n267\t          endpoints.push({\n268\t            path: fullPath,\n269\t            method: httpMethod.toUpperCase(),\n270\t            controller: relativePath,\n271\t            handler: propertyNameText(member.name),\n272\t          });\n273\t        }\n274\t      }\n275\t    }\n276\t  }\n277\t\n278\t  return endpoints;\n279\t}\n280\t\n281\tfunction findDecorator(node: ts.Node, expectedName: string): ts.Decorator | undefined {\n282\t  if (!ts.canHaveDecorators(node)) return undefined;\n283\t  return ts.getDecorators(node)?.find((decorator) => {\n284\t    const expression = decorator.expression;\n285\t    return (\n286\t      ts.isCallExpression(expression) &&\n287\t      ts.isIdentifier(expression.expression) &&\n288\t      expression.expression.text === expectedName\n289\t    );\n290\t  });\n291\t}\n292\t\n293\tfunction decoratorPath(decorator: ts.Decorator): string {\n294\t  const expression = decorator.expression;\n295\t  if (!ts.isCallExpression(expression)) return '';\n296\t  const pathArgument = expression.arguments[0];\n297\t  if (\n298\t    !pathArgument ||\n299\t    (!ts.isStringLiteral(pathArgument) && !ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(pathArgument))\n300\t  ) {\n301\t    return '';\n302\t  }\n303\t  return pathArgument.text;\n304\t}\n305\t\n306\t/**\n307\t * src altindaki tum *.controller.ts dosyalarini recursive bulur.\n308\t */\n309\tfunction findControllerFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n310\t  const results: string[] = [];\n311\t\n312\t  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return results;\n313\t\n314\t  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n315\t  for (const entry of entries) {\n316\t    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);\n317\t    if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n318\t      // node_modules ve test dizinlerini atla\n319\t      if (entry.name !== 'node_modules' && entry.name !== '__tests__') {\n320\t        results.push(...findControllerFiles(fullPath));\n321\t      }\n322\t    } else if (entry.name.endsWith('.controller.ts')) {\n323\t      results.push(fullPath);\n324\t    }\n325\t  }\n326\t\n327\t  return results;\n328\t}\n329\t\n330\t// ============================================================================\n331\t// Path Matching\n332\t// ============================================================================\n333\t\n334\t/**\n335\t * Frontend URL'inin backend endpoint'iyle eslesip eslesmedigini kontrol eder.\n336\t *\n337\t * Frontend: /tenants/:param/notes/:param   (template literal'den normalize edilmis)\n338\t * Backend:  /tenants/:id/notes/:noteId      (NestJS param dekoratoru)\n339\t *\n340\t * Bir segment ya her iki tarafta da parametredir ya da her iki tarafta da\n341\t * AYNI literal'dir. Frontend literal'inin backend parametresine eslesmesi\n342\t * (`/jobs/scheduled` ↔ `/jobs/:id`) bir eslesme DEGIL, tam olarak ADMIN-HIGH-011'in\n343\t * \"route shadowing\" hatasidir: istek `:id` handler'ina duser ve `scheduled`\n344\t * bir kimlik gibi islenir. Bu fonksiyon o durumu artik gecirmez.\n345\t */\n346\tfunction matchPath(frontendUrl: string, backendPath: string): boolean {\n347\t  const feParts = frontendUrl.split('/').filter(Boolean);\n348\t  const beParts = backendPath.split('/').filter(Boolean);\n349\t\n350\t  if (feParts.length !== beParts.length) return false;\n351\t\n352\t  for (let i = 0; i < feParts.length; i++) {\n353\t    const fe = feParts[i]!;\n354\t    const be = beParts[i]!;\n355\t    const feIsParam = fe.startsWith(':');\n356\t    const beIsParam = be.startsWith(':');\n357\t\n358\t    if (feIsParam && beIsParam) continue;\n359\t    if (!feIsParam && !beIsParam && fe === be) continue;\n360\t\n361\t    return false;\n362\t  }\n363\t\n364\t  return true;\n365\t}\n366\t\n367\t/**\n368\t * Iki endpoint'in hem path hem method olarak eslesmesini kontrol eder.\n369\t * PATCH ve PUT her ikisi de eslestirilebilir (frontend PUT kullansa backend PATCH de olabilir).\n370\t */\n371\tfunction matchEndpoint(fe: FrontendEndpoint, be: BackendEndpoint): boolean {\n372\t  if (!matchPath(fe.url, be.path)) return false;\n373\t\n374\t  // Method eslesmesi\n375\t  if (fe.method === be.method) return true;\n376\t\n377\t  // PATCH/PUT uyumlulugu\n378\t  if (\n379\t    (fe.method === 'PATCH' && be.method === 'PUT') ||\n380\t    (fe.method === 'PUT' && be.method === 'PATCH')\n381\t  ) {\n382\t    return true;\n383\t  }\n384\t\n385\t  return false;\n386\t}\n387\t\n388\t// ============================================================================\n389\t// Bilinen istisnalar\n390\t// ============================================================================\n391\t\n392\t/**\n393\t * Bazi frontend endpoint'leri dogrudan backend controller'a eslesmedigi\n394\t * bilinen durumlar. Bu URL'ler test'den hariç tutulur.\n395\t *\n396\t * Her bir istisna icin neden aciklanir.\n397\t */\n398\tconst KNOWN_EXCEPTIONS: Array<{ url: string; method: string; reason: string }> = [\n399\t  // Frontend'de /analytics/geographic var ama backend'de ayri endpoint yok,\n400\t  // analyticsService.getGeographicDistribution() dahili data donduruyor\n401\t  {\n402\t    url: '/analytics/geographic',\n403\t    method: 'GET',\n404\t    reason: 'Backend returns mock data from analytics service, no dedicated controller endpoint',\n405\t  },\n406\t\n407\t  // Frontend export URL'leri: bunlar ADMIN_API_URL ile build edilir, dogrudan download link'i\n408\t  {\n409\t    url: '/reports/export/:param',\n410\t    method: 'GET',\n411\t    reason: 'Export URL builder, maps to /reports/export/pdf/:reportType or /reports/export/csv',\n412\t  },\n413\t  {\n414\t    url: '/reports/export/:param/:param',\n415\t    method: 'GET',\n416\t    reason: 'Export URL with reportType, maps to /reports/export/pdf/:reportType',\n417\t  },\n418\t\n419\t  // Frontend /analytics/usage/api calls backend /analytics/usage + query\n420\t  {\n421\t    url: '/analytics/usage/api',\n422\t    method: 'GET',\n423\t    reason: 'Not a separate endpoint; usage analytics with query params',\n424\t  },\n425\t\n426\t  // Frontend /analytics/engagement* endpoints -- backend returns data from service methods\n427\t  {\n428\t    url: '/analytics/engagement',\n429\t    method: 'GET',\n430\t    reason: 'Analytics engagement data served by usage endpoint or service mock',\n431\t  },\n432\t  {\n433\t    url: '/analytics/engagement/features',\n434\t    method: 'GET',\n435\t    reason: 'Feature engagement data served by usage/features endpoint',\n436\t  },\n437\t\n438\t  // Frontend /database/monitoring/stats -> backend /database/monitoring/health\n439\t  {\n440\t    url: '/database/monitoring/stats',\n441\t    method: 'GET',\n442\t    reason: 'Frontend alias for /database/monitoring/health',\n443\t  },\n444\t\n445\t  // Frontend /database/monitoring/tables -> backend /database/monitoring/storage/by-tenant\n446\t  {\n447\t    url: '/database/monitoring/tables',\n448\t    method: 'GET',\n449\t    reason: 'Frontend alias for /database/monitoring/storage tables',\n450\t  },\n451\t\n452\t  // Frontend /database/monitoring/vacuum and /database/monitoring/analyze\n453\t  {\n454\t    url: '/database/monitoring/vacuum',\n455\t    method: 'POST',\n456\t    reason: 'DB maintenance operations not exposed as REST endpoints',\n457\t  },\n458\t  {\n459\t    url: '/database/monitoring/analyze',\n460\t    method: 'POST',\n461\t    reason: 'DB maintenance operations not exposed as REST endpoints',\n462\t  },\n463\t\n464\t  // Frontend /database/schemas/:param/optimize and /database/schemas/:param/analyze\n465\t  {\n466\t    url: '/database/schemas/:param/optimize',\n467\t    method: 'POST',\n468\t    reason: 'Schema optimization not yet implemented in controller',\n469\t  },\n470\t  {\n471\t    url: '/database/schemas/:param/analyze',\n472\t    method: 'GET',\n473\t    reason: 'Schema analysis not yet implemented in controller',\n474\t  },\n475\t  {\n476\t    url: '/database/schemas/:param/reset',\n477\t    method: 'POST',\n478\t    reason: 'Schema reset not yet implemented in controller',\n479\t  },\n480\t\n481\t  // Database migration frontend vs backend path mismatch\n482\t  {\n483\t    url: '/database/migrations',\n484\t    method: 'GET',\n485\t    reason: 'Frontend expects flat list, backend uses /database/migrations/history',\n486\t  },\n487\t  {\n488\t    url: '/database/migrations/:param',\n489\t    method: 'GET',\n490\t    reason:\n491\t      'Frontend uses migration ID, backend uses /database/migrations/tenant/:tenantId/history',\n492\t  },\n493\t  {\n494\t    url: '/database/migrations',\n495\t    method: 'POST',\n496\t    reason: 'Frontend creates migration, backend uses /database/migrations/tenant/:tenantId/run',\n497\t  },\n498\t  {\n499\t    url: '/database/migrations/:param/run',\n500\t    method: 'POST',\n501\t    reason: 'Frontend runs by ID, backend runs by tenant+version',\n502\t  },\n503\t  {\n504\t    url: '/database/migrations/:param/rollback',\n505\t    method: 'POST',\n506\t    reason: 'Frontend rollback by ID, backend by tenant+version',\n507\t  },\n508\t  {\n509\t    url: '/database/migrations/pending',\n510\t    method: 'GET',\n511\t    reason: 'Frontend list pending, backend /database/migrations/tenant/:tenantId/pending',\n512\t  },\n513\t\n514\t  // Security activities export - frontend uses GET with query, backend uses POST\n515\t  {\n516\t    url: frontend path\n522\t  {\n523\t    url: '/security/activities/user/:param',\n524\t    method: 'GET',\n525\t    reason: 'Not a controller endpoint; activity query with userId filter',\n526\t  },\n527\t\n528\t  // Security audit entity path\n529\t  {\n530\t    url: '/security/audit/entity/:param/:param',\n531\t    method: 'GET',\n532\t    reason:\n533\t      'Audit trail entity query not as '/security/audit/retention-policies/:param/run',\n539\t    method: 'POST',\n540\t    reason: 'Backend uses /security/audit/retention-policies/apply POST instead',\n541\t  },\n542\t\n543\t  // backendEndpoints.length;\n945\t\n946\t    // 601: was 603 (+1 for GET /billing/payments/stats, ADMIN-HIGH-008; -1 for\n947\t    // GET /impersonation/stats, deleted as the all-time twin of\n948\t    // GET /impersonation/audit/summary, ADMIN-MEDIUM-084 — the two messaging\n949\t    // endpoints touched by #962 already existed as 501 throws, so the route\n950\t    // count did not move for them). -2 for the two\n951\t    // `/security/activities/sessions/user/:userId*` routes, which read and\n952\t    // wrote `admin.user_sessions`, a table this service never populated: the\n953\t    // read always answered `[]` and the terminate always answered\n954\t    // `{ terminated: 0 }` while every real session stayed live. Session state\n955\t    // is auth-service's, reachable at GET /users/:id/sessions and\n956\t    // PATCH /users/:id/force-logout (ADMIN-HIGH-100).\n957\t    // 590: -11 for the /database/backups surface, deleted with the pg_dump\n958\t    // backup subsystem WAL-G already replaced (INFRA-CRITICAL-164).\n959\t    // 585: -5 for the runtime retention-policy CRUD, replaced by the\n960\t    // registry's read-only view (DATA-CRITICAL-016).\n961\t    // 539: -46 for the /impersonation/* and /debug-tools/* surfaces, deleted\n962\t    // with the subsystem that had no consumer (SEC-CRITICAL-162). The audit\n963\t    // wave counted 47 there; main had already deleted GET /impersonation/stats\n964\t    // as the all-time twin of the audit summary (ADMIN-MEDIUM-084), so one of\n965\t    // the 47 was gone before this branch touched it.\n966\t    // 538: -1 for POST /security/activities, whose body named the actor\n967\t    // through userId / userName / userEmail / ipAddress (ADMIN-CRITICAL-102).\n968\t    // 526: -12 for the twelve routes of the settings IP allow-list surface,\n969\t    // deleted with both access-rule stacks — the admin-api CRUD wrote a table\n970\t    // no guard read, and the gateway guard was registered nowhere\n971\t    // (SEC-HIGH-165).\n972\t    // 529: +3 for the platform-capability grant surface, which narrows the\n973\t    // SUPER_ADMIN bit to named capabilities (SEC-HIGH-164).\n974\t    // 463: -66 for every route that existed only to refuse — the\n975\t    // tenant-configuration stack, the system-settings writers, the\n976\t    // global-config CRUD, the 409 subscription/schema/migration refusals and\n977\t    // the 501 tenant-limit query, deleted with their service methods, DTOs,\n978\t    // clients and page controls (ADMIN-HIGH-106). The two messaging 501s the\n979\t    // audit counted here are NOT in that number: main answered them with real\n980\t    // cross-tenant aggregates before this landed (ADMIN-HIGH-009), and a route\n981\t    // that returns real data is not a stub.\n982\t    // 462: -1 for POST /billing/plans/seed. Seeding the plan catalogue is\n983\t    // billing's own boot-time concern once `billing.plans` is the only\n984\t    // catalogue, not an admin route (ADR-0013 / BILLING-CRITICAL-011). The\n985\t    // discount and module-price surfaces moved in the same wave and are NOT in\n986\t    // this number: their rows moved to billing, but admin-api keeps every route\n987\t    // — it reads the rows read-only and forwards each write as a command, so\n988\t    // the operator surface is unchanged and only its backing store moved.\n989\t    // 461: -1 for POST /security/monitoring/analyze/login. Anomaly detection\n990\t    // ran only when a SUPER_ADMIN pressed a button; it now runs from the\n991\t    // `events.security.events.auth.login.*` stream on every real attempt, so\n992\t    // the route had nothing left to trigger (ADMIN-HIGH-109).\n993\t    // 460: -1 for POST /system/errors/report, whose replacement is the\n994\t    // `events.*.ServiceErrorCaptured` stream every service publishes: a defect\n995\t    // reaches the store because it happened, not because something remembered\n996\t    // to POST it. The audit wave counted -3 here, expecting to retire\n997\t    // `admin.user_sessions`' two session routes in the same change; main had\n998\t    // already deleted them (ADMIN-HIGH-100), so they are in the baseline and\n999\t    // only this one moves.\n1000\t    // 459: -1 for POST /system/performance/metrics/request. `recordRequestMetric`\n1001\t    // drained an in-memory map nothing ever filled — the route had no caller and\n1002\t    // the RED data it duplicated is already in Prometheus, so it goes with the\n1003\t    // aggregation cron that read it (ADMIN-HIGH-109).\n1004\t    expect(count).toBe(459);\n1005\t  });\n1006\t\n1007\t  it('frontend endpoint snapshot should be up to date', () => {\n1008\t    const count = frontendEndpoints.length;\n1009\t\n1010\t    // Frontend'den ~200-350 endpoint bekleniyor\n1011\t    expect(count).toBeGreaterThan(150);\n1012\t    expect(count).toBeLessThan(500);\n1013\t  });\n1014\t\n1015\t  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n1018\t\n1019\t  it('should report all known exceptions for review', () => {\n1020\t    // Bu test her zaman basarili olur ama eslesmeyenleri loglar\n1021\t    const unmatched: string[] = [];\n1022\t\n1023\t    for (const fe of frontendEndpoints) {\n1024\t      if (isKnownException(fe)) continue;\n1025\t\n1026\t      const hasMatch = backendEndpoints.some((be) => matchEndpoint(fe, be));\n1027\t      if (!hasMatch) {\n1028\t        unmatched.push(`${fe.method} ${fe.url} (${fe.source}::${fe.functionName})`);\n1029\t      }\n1030\t    }\n1031\t\n1032\t    // Eslesmeyenlerin sayisi 0 olmali (tum eslesmeyenler bilinen istisnalarda olmali)\n1033\t    expect(unmatched).toEqual([]);\n1034\t  });\n1035\t});\n1036\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"832aee52-fb2a-4eef-bd51-8f889bd990f2","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:20:17.857Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/contract-validation.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Frontend-Backend Contract Validation Test\n *\n * Sprint 4 Fix - Grup Y (H12/46)\n * ---------------------------------\n * Bu test, admin-panel frontend'inin cagirdigi API endpoint'leri ile\n * admin-api-service backend'inin sundugu endpoint'lerin uyumunu dogrular.\n *\n * Problem: 3 FIELD_MISMATCH production'da 400/404 uretiyordu ama CI/CD yakalayamiyordu.\n * Cozum: Statik analiz ile frontend URL pattern'lerini ve backend controller path'lerini\n *         extract edip, eslesme kontrolu yapan otomatik test.\n *\n * Yaklasim:\n * - Frontend: services/api/*.ts dosyalarindan apiFetch cagrilarindaki URL pattern'leri cikarilir\n * - Backend: Controller dekoratorlerinden (@Get, @Post, vb.) endpoint path'leri cikarilir\n * - Eslesme: Her frontend URL'in backend'de bir karsiligi olmali\n *\n * Bakim notu: Yeni endpoint eklendiginde veya mevcut path degistiginde bu test kirilir.\n * Bu beklenen ve istenen davranistir -- kontrat degisiklikleri bilinmeli.\n */\n\nimport * as fs from 'fs';\nimport * as path from 'path';\nimport * as ts from 'typescript';\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Types\n// ============================================================================\n\ninterface FrontendEndpoint {\n  url: string;\n  method: string;\n  source: string; // api dosya adi\n  functionName: string;\n}\n\ninterface BackendEndpoint {\n  path: string;\n  method: string;\n  controller: string;\n  handler: string;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Frontend URL Extraction\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Frontend API dosyalarindan apiFetch cagrilarindaki URL pattern'lerini cikarir.\n * Template literal'ler normalize edilir: ${param} -> :param\n */\nfunction extractFrontendEndpoints(): FrontendEndpoint[] {\n  const apiDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../../../web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api');\n\n  if (!fs.existsSync(apiDir)) {\n    throw new Error(`Frontend API directory not found: ${apiDir}`);\n  }\n\n  const endpoints: FrontendEndpoint[] = [];\n  const apiFiles = fs.readdirSync(apiDir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.ts'));\n\n  for (const file of apiFiles) {\n    const filePath = path.join(apiDir, file);\n    const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');\n    const source = file.replace('.ts', '');\n    const sourceFile = ts.createSourceFile(\n      filePath,\n      content,\n      ts.ScriptTarget.Latest,\n      true,\n      ts.ScriptKind.TS,\n    );\n\n    const visit = (node: ts.Node): void => {\n      if (\n        ts.isCallExpression(node) &&\n        ts.isIdentifier(node.expression) &&\n        node.expression.text === 'apiFetch'\n      ) {\n        const urlArgument = node.arguments[0];\n        const rawUrl = urlArgument ? renderStaticUrl(urlArgument) : null;\n\n        // HATEOAS links validated and returned by the backend are deliberately\n        // dynamic. They are not a second declaration of a route, so there is\n        // no static frontend route to compare for those calls.\n        if (rawUrl !== null) {\n          const url = normalizeUrl(rawUrl);\n          const method = extractMethod(node.arguments[1]);\n          const functionName = findContainingFunctionName(node);\n\n          if (\n            url &&\n            !endpoints.some(\n              (endpoint) =>\n                endpoint.url === url && endpoint.method === method && endpoint.source === source,\n            )\n          ) {\n            endpoints.push({ url, method, source, functionName });\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      ts.forEachChild(node, visit);\n    };\n\n    visit(sourceFile);\n  }\n\n  return endpoints;\n}\n\nfunction renderStaticUrl(expression: ts.Expression): string | null {\n  if (ts.isStringLiteral(expression) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(expression)) {\n    return expression.text;\n  }\n\n  if (!ts.isTemplateExpression(expression)) return null;\n\n  let rendered = expression.head.text;\n  for (const span of expression.templateSpans) {\n    if (expressionIntroducesQuery(span.expression)) {\n      rendered += '?';\n      break;\n    }\n\n    // A template whose first segment is a runtime path (for example a\n    // backend-provided status URL) is not a statically declared route.\n    if (rendered === '' && ts.isIdentifier(span.expression)) return null;\n\n    rendered += ':param';\n    rendered += span.literal.text;\n  }\n  return rendered;\n}\n\nfunction expressionIntroducesQuery(expression: ts.Expression): boolean {\n  let introducesQuery = false;\n  const visit = (node: ts.Node): void => {\n    if (ts.isTemplateExpression(node) && node.head.text.startsWith('?')) {\n      introducesQuery = true;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (\n      (ts.isStringLiteral(node) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(node)) &&\n      node.text.startsWith('?')\n    ) {\n      introducesQuery = true;\n      return;\n    }\n    ts.forEachChild(node, visit);\n  };\n  visit(expression);\n  return introducesQuery;\n}\n\nfunction findContainingFunctionName(node: ts.Node): string {\n  let current: ts.Node | undefined = node.parent;\n  while (current) {\n    if (ts.isPropertyAssignment(current) || ts.isMethodDeclaration(current)) {\n      return propertyNameText(current.name);\n    }\n    if (ts.isVariableDeclaration(current) && ts.isIdentifier(current.name)) {\n      return current.name.text;\n    }\n    current = current.parent;\n  }\n  return 'unknown';\n}\n\nfunction propertyNameText(name: ts.PropertyName | undefined): string {\n  if (!name) return 'unknown';\n  if (ts.isIdentifier(name) || ts.isStringLiteral(name) || ts.isNumericLiteral(name)) {\n    return name.text;\n  }\n  return name.getText();\n}\n\n/**\n * URL'i normalize eder:\n * - Query string'leri (?...) kaldirir\n * - Template literal parametrelerini (${...}) :param'a cevirir\n * - Trailing slash kaldirir\n * - encodeURIComponent(...) kaldır\n */\nfunction normalizeUrl(rawUrl: string): string {\n  // Query string'i kaldir\n  let url = rawUrl.split('?')[0]!;\n\n  // ${buildQueryString(...)} kaldir\n  url = url.replace(/\\$\\{buildQueryString\\([^)]*\\)\\}/g, '');\n\n  // ${encodeURIComponent(...)} -> :param\n  url = url.replace(/\\$\\{encodeURIComponent\\([^)]*\\)\\}/g, ':param');\n\n  // Template literal parametrelerini :param'a cevir\n  // ${variable} veya ${variable.property} veya ${expression}\n  url = url.replace(/\\$\\{[^}]+\\}/g, ':param');\n\n  // Trailing slash kaldir\n  url = url.replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  // Bos path segment'leri temizle\n  url = url.replace(/\\/+/g, '/');\n\n  return url;\n}\n\n/**\n * apiFetch cagrisindaki HTTP method'u cikarir.\n * Varsayilan: GET\n */\nfunction extractMethod(options: ts.Expression | undefined): string {\n  if (!options || !ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(options)) return 'GET';\n\n  for (const property of options.properties) {\n    if (\n      ts.isPropertyAssignment(property) &&\n      propertyNameText(property.name) === 'method' &&\n      ts.isStringLiteral(property.initializer)\n    ) {\n      return property.initializer.text.toUpperCase();\n    }\n  }\n  return 'GET';\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Backend Endpoint Extraction\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Backend controller dosyalarindan decorator-tabanli endpoint path'lerini cikarir.\n * @Controller('prefix') + @Get('sub') => /prefix/sub\n */\nfunction extractBackendEndpoints(): BackendEndpoint[] {\n  const srcDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');\n  const endpoints: BackendEndpoint[] = [];\n\n  // Controller dosyalarini bul\n  const controllerFiles = findControllerFiles(srcDir);\n\n  for (const filePath of controllerFiles) {\n    const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');\n    const relativePath = path.relative(srcDir, filePath);\n    const sourceFile = ts.createSourceFile(\n      filePath,\n      content,\n      ts.ScriptTarget.Latest,\n      true,\n      ts.ScriptKind.TS,\n    );\n\n    for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n      if (!ts.isClassDeclaration(statement)) continue;\n      const controller = findDecorator(statement, 'Controller');\n      if (!controller) continue;\n      const controllerPrefix = decoratorPath(controller);\n\n      for (const member of statement.members) {\n        if (!ts.isMethodDeclaration(member)) continue;\n        for (const httpMethod of ['Get', 'Post', 'Put', 'Patch', 'Delete'] as const) {\n          const decorator = findDecorator(member, httpMethod);\n          if (!decorator) continue;\n          const subPath = decoratorPath(decorator);\n          const fullPath = normalizeUrl(`/${controllerPrefix}/${subPath}`);\n\n          endpoints.push({\n            path: fullPath,\n            method: httpMethod.toUpperCase(),\n            controller: relativePath,\n            handler: propertyNameText(member.name),\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return endpoints;\n}\n\nfunction findDecorator(node: ts.Node, expectedName: string): ts.Decorator | undefined {\n  if (!ts.canHaveDecorators(node)) return undefined;\n  return ts.getDecorators(node)?.find((decorator) => {\n    const expression = decorator.expression;\n    return (\n      ts.isCallExpression(expression) &&\n      ts.isIdentifier(expression.expression) &&\n      expression.expression.text === expectedName\n    );\n  });\n}\n\nfunction decoratorPath(decorator: ts.Decorator): string {\n  const expression = decorator.expression;\n  if (!ts.isCallExpression(expression)) return '';\n  const pathArgument = expression.arguments[0];\n  if (\n    !pathArgument ||\n    (!ts.isStringLiteral(pathArgument) && !ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(pathArgument))\n  ) {\n    return '';\n  }\n  return pathArgument.text;\n}\n\n/**\n * src altindaki tum *.controller.ts dosyalarini recursive bulur.\n */\nfunction findControllerFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n  const results: string[] = [];\n\n  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return results;\n\n  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n  for (const entry of entries) {\n    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);\n    if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n      // node_modules ve test dizinlerini atla\n      if (entry.name !== 'node_modules' && entry.name !== '__tests__') {\n        results.push(...findControllerFiles(fullPath));\n      }\n    } else if (entry.name.endsWith('.controller.ts')) {\n      results.push(fullPath);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return results;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Path Matching\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Frontend URL'inin backend endpoint'iyle eslesip eslesmedigini kontrol eder.\n *\n * Frontend: /tenants/:param/notes/:param   (template literal'den normalize edilmis)\n * Backend:  /tenants/:id/notes/:noteId      (NestJS param dekoratoru)\n *\n * Bir segment ya her iki tarafta da parametredir ya da her iki tarafta da\n * AYNI literal'dir. Frontend literal'inin backend parametresine eslesmesi\n * (`/jobs/scheduled` ↔ `/jobs/:id`) bir eslesme DEGIL, tam olarak ADMIN-HIGH-011'in\n * \"route shadowing\" hatasidir: istek `:id` handler'ina duser ve `scheduled`\n * bir kimlik gibi islenir. Bu fonksiyon o durumu artik gecirmez.\n */\nfunction matchPath(frontendUrl: string, backendPath: string): boolean {\n  const feParts = frontendUrl.split('/').filter(Boolean);\n  const beParts = backendPath.split('/').filter(Boolean);\n\n  if (feParts.length !== beParts.length) return false;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < feParts.length; i++) {\n    const fe = feParts[i]!;\n    const be = beParts[i]!;\n    const feIsParam = fe.startsWith(':');\n    const beIsParam = be.startsWith(':');\n\n    if (feIsParam && beIsParam) continue;\n    if (!feIsParam && !beIsParam && fe === be) continue;\n\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * Iki endpoint'in hem path hem method olarak eslesmesini kontrol eder.\n * PATCH ve PUT her ikisi de eslestirilebilir (frontend PUT kullansa backend PATCH de olabilir).\n */\nfunction matchEndpoint(fe: FrontendEndpoint, be: BackendEndpoint): boolean {\n  if (!matchPath(fe.url, be.path)) return false;\n\n  // Method eslesmesi\n  if (fe.method === be.method) return true;\n\n  // PATCH/PUT uyumlulugu\n  if (\n    (fe.method === 'PATCH' && be.method === 'PUT') ||\n    (fe.method === 'PUT' && be.method === 'PATCH')\n  ) {\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  return false;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Bilinen istisnalar\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Bazi frontend endpoint'leri dogrudan backend controller'a eslesmedigi\n * bilinen durumlar. Bu URL'ler test'den hariç tutulur.\n *\n * Her bir istisna icin neden aciklanir.\n */\nconst KNOWN_EXCEPTIONS: Array<{ url: string; method: string; reason: string }> = [\n  // Frontend'de /analytics/geographic var ama backend'de ayri endpoint yok,\n  // analyticsService.getGeographicDistribution() dahili data donduruyor\n  {\n    url: '/analytics/geographic',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Backend returns mock data from analytics service, no dedicated controller endpoint',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend export URL'leri: bunlar ADMIN_API_URL ile build edilir, dogrudan download link'i\n  {\n    url: '/reports/export/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Export URL builder, maps to /reports/export/pdf/:reportType or /reports/export/csv',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/reports/export/:param/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Export URL with reportType, maps to /reports/export/pdf/:reportType',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /analytics/usage/api calls backend /analytics/usage + query\n  {\n    url: '/analytics/usage/api',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Not a separate endpoint; usage analytics with query params',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /analytics/engagement* endpoints -- backend returns data from service methods\n  {\n    url: '/analytics/engagement',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Analytics engagement data served by usage endpoint or service mock',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/analytics/engagement/features',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Feature engagement data served by usage/features endpoint',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /database/monitoring/stats -> backend /database/monitoring/health\n  {\n    url: '/database/monitoring/stats',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Frontend alias for /database/monitoring/health',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /database/monitoring/tables -> backend /database/monitoring/storage/by-tenant\n  {\n    url: '/database/monitoring/tables',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Frontend alias for /database/monitoring/storage tables',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /database/monitoring/vacuum and /database/monitoring/analyze\n  {\n    url: '/database/monitoring/vacuum',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'DB maintenance operations not exposed as REST endpoints',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/monitoring/analyze',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'DB maintenance operations not exposed as REST endpoints',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /database/schemas/:param/optimize and /database/schemas/:param/analyze\n  {\n    url: '/database/schemas/:param/optimize',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Schema optimization not yet implemented in controller',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/schemas/:param/analyze',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Schema analysis not yet implemented in controller',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/schemas/:param/reset',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Schema reset not yet implemented in controller',\n  },\n\n  // Database migration frontend vs backend path mismatch\n  {\n    url: '/database/migrations',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Frontend expects flat list, backend uses /database/migrations/history',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/migrations/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason:\n      'Frontend uses migration ID, backend uses /database/migrations/tenant/:tenantId/history',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/migrations',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Frontend creates migration, backend uses /database/migrations/tenant/:tenantId/run',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/migrations/:param/run',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Frontend runs by ID, backend runs by tenant+version',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/migrations/:param/rollback',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Frontend rollback by ID, backend by tenant+version',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/database/migrations/pending',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Frontend list pending, backend /database/migrations/tenant/:tenantId/pending',\n  },\n\n  // Security activities export - frontend uses GET with query, backend uses POST\n  {\n    url: '/security/activities/export',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Frontend uses GET, backend audit trail uses POST export',\n  },\n\n  // Security activities/user/:userId - frontend path\n  {\n    url: '/security/activities/user/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Not a controller endpoint; activity query with userId filter',\n  },\n\n  // Security audit entity path\n  {\n    url: '/security/audit/entity/:param/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason:\n      'Audit trail entity query not as controller endpoint; use main query with entityType/entityId',\n  },\n\n  // Security audit retention policy run by ID\n  {\n    url: '/security/audit/retention-policies/:param/run',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Backend uses /security/audit/retention-policies/apply POST instead',\n  },\n\n  // Compliance dashboard\n  {\n    url: '/security/compliance/dashboard',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'No dedicated dashboard endpoint; use checks and reports',\n  },\n\n  // Compliance reports generate\n  {\n    url: '/security/compliance/reports/generate',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Frontend path differs from backend /security/compliance/reports POST',\n  },\n\n  // Security monitoring events resolve\n  {\n    url: '/security/monitoring/events/:param/resolve',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Backend uses PUT /security/monitoring/events/:id/status instead',\n  },\n\n  // Security monitoring incidents create/update/timeline\n  {\n    url: '/security/monitoring/incidents',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Incidents are auto-created from events, not manually via POST',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/security/monitoring/incidents/:param/timeline',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Timeline entries are auto-added, not via dedicated endpoint',\n  },\n\n  // Security monitoring threat-intelligence block/unblock\n  {\n    url: '/security/monitoring/threat-intelligence/:param/block',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Block/unblock not separate endpoints in controller',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/security/monitoring/threat-intelligence/:param/unblock',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Block/unblock not separate endpoints in controller',\n  },\n\n  // Security monitoring health-score (frontend path vs controller)\n  {\n    url: '/security/monitoring/health-score',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Backend uses /security/monitoring/health-score (matches)',\n  },\n\n  // Feature toggle key lookup\n  {\n    url: '/system/settings/feature-toggles/key/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'No dedicated key-based lookup in controller; use query with search',\n  },\n\n  // Feature toggle toggle action\n  {\n    url: '/system/settings/feature-toggles/:param/toggle',\n not in global-settings controller',\n  },\n  {\n    url: '/system/jobs/queues/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Job queue detail not in global-settings controller',\n  },\n  {\n    url: in global-settings controller' },\n  {\n    url: '/system/jobs/:param',\n    method: 'GET',\n    reason: 'Job detail not in global-settings controller',\n  },\n  { url: '/system/jobs', method: 'POST', reason: 'Job creation not in global-settings controller' },\n  {\n    url: '/system/jobs/:param/cancel',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Job cancel not in global-settings controller',\n  },\n  {\n '/settings/tenant/:param/webhooks/:param/test',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Webhook test endpoint not in controller',\n  },\n\n  // Frontend /support/tickets PATCH vs backend PUT\n  {\n    url: '/support/tickets/:param/close',\n    method: 'POST',\n    reason: 'Backend uses status change via POST /status with status=closed',\n  },\n];\n\n/**\n * Bilinen istisna mi kontrol eder.\n */\nfunction isKnownException(fe: FrontendEndpoint): boolean {\n  return KNOWN_EXCEPTIONS.some((exc) => matchPath(fe.url, exc.url) && fe.method === exc.method);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tests\n// ============================================================================\n\ndescribe('Frontend-Backend Contract Validation', () => {\n  let frontendEndpoints: FrontendEndpoint[];\n  let backendEndpoints: BackendEndpoint[];\n\n  beforeAll(() => {\n    frontendEndpoints = extractFrontendEndpoints();\n    backendEndpoints = extractBackendEndpoints();\n  });\n\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Temel saglik kontrolleri\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  it('should extract frontend endpoints', () => {\n    expect(frontendEndpoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    // En az 50 endpoint bekleniyor (14 dosya, her biri birden fazla endpoint)\n    expect(frontendEndpoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(50);\n  });\n\n  it('should extract backend endpoints', () => {\n    expect(backendEndpoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(backendEndpoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(50);\n  });\n\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Domain bazli kontrat testleri\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  const domainTests = [\n    { domain: 'system', description: 'System Metrics API' },\n    { domain: 'analytics', description: 'Analytics API' },\n    { domain: 'tenants', description: 'Tenants API' },\n    { domain: 'users', description: 'Users API' },\n    { domain: 'modules', description: 'Modules API' },\n    { domain: 'audit-logs', description: 'Audit Logs API' },\n    { domain: 'billing', description: 'Billing API' },\n    { domain: 'reports', description: 'Reports API' },\n    { domain: 'support', description: 'Support API' },\n    { domain: 'settings', description: 'Settings API' },\n    { domain: 'security', description: 'Security API' },\n    { domain: 'health', description: 'Health API' },\n    { domain: 'database', description: 'Database Management API' },\n  ];\n\n  for (const { domain, description } of domainTests) {\n    it(`should have backend endpoints for ${description} (/${domain}/*)`, () => {\n      const domainFrontend = frontendEndpoints.filter(\n        (fe) => fe.url.startsWith(`/${domain}/`) || fe.url === `/${domain}`,\n      );\n\n      // Bos domain'leri atla\n      if (domainFrontend.length === 0) return;\n\n      const mismatches: string[] = [];\n\n      for (const fe of domainFrontend) {\n        if (isKnownException(fe)) continue;\n\n        const hasMatch = backendEndpoints.some((be) => matchEndpoint(fe, be));\n        if (!hasMatch) {\n          mismatches.push(`  ${fe.method} ${fe.url} (from ${fe.source}.ts::${fe.functionName})`);\n        }\n      }\n\n      if (mismatches.length > 0) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `${mismatches.length} frontend endpoint(s) in /${domain}/* have no backend match:\\n${mismatches.join('\\n')}`,\n        );\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Kritik endpoint'ler icin ozel testler (H12 bulgulari)\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  describe('H12 Critical Path Endpoints', () => {\n    it('live settings email test route should match', () => {\n      const fe = frontendEndpoints.find(\n        (e) => e.url === '/settings/config/email/test' && e.method === 'POST',\n      );\n      expect(fe).toBeDefined();\n\n      const be = backendEndpoints.find(\n        (e) => e.path === '/settings/config/email/test' && e.method === 'POST',\n      );\n      expect(be).toBeDefined();\n    });\n\n    it('announcement unpublish should use /cancel path', () => {\n      // H18 fix: frontend unpublish -> /support/announcements/:id/cancel\n      const fe = frontendEndpoints.find(\n        (e) => e.url === '/support/announcements/:param/cancel' && e.method === 'POST',\n      );\n      expect(fe).toBeDefined();\n\n      const be = backendEndpoints.find(\n        (e) => matchPath(e.path, '/support/announcements/:id/cancel') && e.method === 'POST',\n      );\n      expect(be).toBeDefined();\n    });\n  });\n\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // HTTP method uyumlulugu\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  it('should not have method mismatches for matched paths', () => {\n    const methodMismatches: string[] = [];\n\n    for (const fe of frontendEndpoints) {\n      if (isKnownException(fe)) continue;\n\n      // Ayni path'e sahip ama farkli method'lu backend endpoint var mi?\n      const pathMatches = backendEndpoints.filter((be) => matchPath(fe.url, be.path));\n      if (pathMatches.length === 0) continue; // Path eslesmediyse zaten baska test yakalayacak\n\n      const methodMatch = pathMatches.some((be) => {\n        if (be.method === fe.method) return true;\n        // PATCH/PUT uyumlulugu\n        if (\n          (fe.method === 'PATCH' && be.method === 'PUT') ||\n          (fe.method === 'PUT' && be.method === 'PATCH')\n        ) {\n          return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n      });\n\n      if (!methodMatch) {\n        const beMethods = pathMatches.map((be) => be.method).join(',');\n        methodMismatches.push(\n          `  ${fe.method} ${fe.url} -> backend has [${beMethods}] (from ${fe.source}.ts)`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (methodMismatches.length > 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `${methodMismatches.length} HTTP method mismatch(es):\\n${methodMismatches.join('\\n')}`,\n      );\n    }\n  });\n\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Snapshot: endpoint listesinin snapshot'ini tut\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  it('backend endpoint snapshot should be up to date', () => {\n    // Backend endpoint sayisi belirli bir aralikte olmali.\n    // Yeni endpoint eklendiginde veya kaldirildiginda bu test guncellenmeli.\n    // Bu, beklenmedik endpoint degisikliklerini yakalar.\n    const count = backendEndpoints.length;\n\n    // 601: was 603 (+1 for GET /billing/payments/stats, ADMIN-HIGH-008; -1 for\n    // GET /impersonation/stats, deleted as the all-time twin of\n    // GET /impersonation/audit/summary, ADMIN-MEDIUM-084 — the two messaging\n    // endpoints touched by #962 already existed as 501 throws, so the route\n    // count did not move for them). -2 for the two\n    // `/security/activities/sessions/user/:userId*` routes, which read and\n    // wrote `admin.user_sessions`, a table this service never populated: the\n    // read always answered `[]` and the terminate always answered\n    // `{ terminated: 0 }` while every real session stayed live. Session state\n    // is auth-service's, reachable at GET /users/:id/sessions and\n    // PATCH /users/:id/force-logout (ADMIN-HIGH-100).\n    // 590: -11 for the /database/backups surface, deleted with the pg_dump\n    // backup subsystem WAL-G already replaced (INFRA-CRITICAL-164).\n    // 585: -5 for the runtime retention-policy CRUD, replaced by the\n    // registry's read-only view (DATA-CRITICAL-016).\n    // 539: -46 for the /impersonation/* and /debug-tools/* surfaces, deleted\n    // with the subsystem that had no consumer (SEC-CRITICAL-162). The audit\n    // wave counted 47 there; main had already deleted GET /impersonation/stats\n    // as the all-time twin of the audit summary (ADMIN-MEDIUM-084), so one of\n    // the 47 was gone before this branch touched it.\n    // 538: -1 for POST /security/activities, whose body named the actor\n    // through userId / userName / userEmail / ipAddress (ADMIN-CRITICAL-102).\n    // 526: -12 for the twelve routes of the settings IP allow-list surface,\n    // deleted with both access-rule stacks — the admin-api CRUD wrote a table\n    // no guard read, and the gateway guard was registered nowhere\n    // (SEC-HIGH-165).\n    // 529: +3 for the platform-capability grant surface, which narrows the\n    // SUPER_ADMIN bit to named capabilities (SEC-HIGH-164).\n    // 463: -66 for every route that existed only to refuse — the\n    // tenant-configuration stack, the system-settings writers, the\n    // global-config CRUD, the 409 subscription/schema/migration refusals and\n    // the 501 tenant-limit query, deleted with their service methods, DTOs,\n    // clients and page controls (ADMIN-HIGH-106). The two messaging 501s the\n    // audit counted here are NOT in that number: main answered them with real\n    // cross-tenant aggregates before this landed (ADMIN-HIGH-009), and a route\n    // that returns real data is not a stub.\n    // 462: -1 for POST /billing/plans/seed. Seeding the plan catalogue is\n    // billing's own boot-time concern once `billing.plans` is the only\n    // catalogue, not an admin route (ADR-0013 / BILLING-CRITICAL-011). The\n    // discount and module-price surfaces moved in the same wave and are NOT in\n    // this number: their rows moved to billing, but admin-api keeps every route\n    // — it reads the rows read-only and forwards each write as a command, so\n    // the operator surface is unchanged and only its backing store moved.\n    // 461: -1 for POST /security/monitoring/analyze/login. Anomaly detection\n    // ran only when a SUPER_ADMIN pressed a button; it now runs from the\n    // `events.security.events.auth.login.*` stream on every real attempt, so\n    // the route had nothing left to trigger (ADMIN-HIGH-109).\n    // 460: -1 for POST /system/errors/report, whose replacement is the\n    // `events.*.ServiceErrorCaptured` stream every service publishes: a defect\n    // reaches the store because it happened, not because something remembered\n    // to POST it. The audit wave counted -3 here, expecting to retire\n    // `admin.user_sessions`' two session routes in the same change; main had\n    // already deleted them (ADMIN-HIGH-100), so they are in the baseline and\n    // only this one moves.\n    // 459: -1 for POST /system/performance/metrics/request. `recordRequestMetric`\n    // drained an in-memory map nothing ever filled — the route had no caller and\n    // the RED data it duplicated is already in Prometheus, so it goes with the\n    // aggregation cron that read it (ADMIN-HIGH-109).\n    expect(count).toBe(459);\n  });\n\n  it('frontend endpoint snapshot should be up to date', () => {\n    const count = frontendEndpoints.length;\n\n    // Frontend'den ~200-350 endpoint bekleniyor\n    expect(count).toBeGreaterThan(150);\n    expect(count).toBeLessThan(500);\n  });\n\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Raporlama: eslesmeyenleri listele (bilgi amacli, test basarisiz degilse)\n  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  it('should report all known exceptions for review', () => {\n    // Bu test her zaman basarili olur ama eslesmeyenleri loglar\n    const unmatched: string[] = [];\n\n    for (const fe of frontendEndpoints) {\n      if (isKnownException(fe)) continue;\n\n      const hasMatch = backendEndpoints.some((be) => matchEndpoint(fe, be));\n      if (!hasMatch) {\n        unmatched.push(`${fe.method} ${fe.url} (${fe.source}::${fe.functionName})`);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Eslesmeyenlerin sayisi 0 olmali (tum eslesmeyenler bilinen istisnalarda olmali)\n    expect(unmatched).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":1036,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1036}}}

const key = 'custom:limit';\n62\t      const limit = 3;\n63\t      const windowMs = 60000;\n64\t\n65\t      const r1 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n66\t
