Basic Consume\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('consume()', () => {\n    it('should allow requests under the limit', async () => {\n      const result = await strategy.consume('test:key');\n      expect(result.allowed).toBe(true);\n      expect(result.remaining).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n    });\n\n    it('should track remaining requests', async () => {\n      const r1 = await strategy.consume('remaining:key');\n      const r2 = await strategy.consume('remaining:key');\n\n      expect(r2.remaining).toBeLessThan(r1.remaining);\n    });\n\n    it('should return resetTime as a Date', async () => {\n      const result = await strategy.consume('date:key');\n      expect(result.resetTime).toBeInstanceOf(Date);\n      expect(result.resetTime.getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 2. consumeWithConfig - Custom Limits\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('consumeWithConfig()', () => {\n    it('should allow requests up to custom limit', async () => {\n      const key = 'custom:limit';\n      const limit = 3;\n      const windowMs = 60000;\n\n      const r1 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n      expect(r1.allowed).toBe(true);\n      expect(r1.remaining).toBe(2);\n\n      const r2 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n      expect(r2.allowed).toBe(true);\n      expect(r2.remaining).toBe(1);\n\n      const r3 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n      expect(r3.allowed).toBe(true);\n      expect(r3.remaining).toBe(0);\n\n      // 4th should be blocked\n      const r4 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n      expect(r4.allowed).toBe(false);\n      expect(r4.remaining).toBe(0);\n      expect(r4.retryAfter).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    });\n\n    it('should return retryAfter in seconds when blocked', async () => {\n      const key = 'retry:after';\n      const limit = 1;\n      const windowMs = 30000;\n\n      await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n      const result = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, limit, windowMs);\n\n      expect(result.allowed).toBe(false);\n      expect(result.retryAfter).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n      expect(result.retryAfter).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30); // Within window\n    });\n\n    it('should use separate tracking for different keys', async () => {\n      const r1 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig('key-a', 1, 60000);\n      const r2 = await strategy.consumeWithConfig('key-b', 1, 60000);\n\n      expect(r1.allowed).toBe(true);\n      expect(r2.allowed).toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 3. Reset\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('reset()', () => {\n    it('should clear rate limit for a key', async () => {\n      const key = 'reset:key';\n\n      // Exhaust the limit\n      await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, 1, 60000);\n      const blocked = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, 1, 60000);\n      expect(blocked.allowed).toBe(false);\n\n      // Reset\n      await strategy.reset(key);\n\n      // Should be allowed again\n      const afterReset = await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, 1, 60000);\n      expect(afterReset.allowed).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should not affect other keys when resetting one', async () => {\n      await strategy.consumeWithConfig('key-x', 1, 60000);\n      await strategy.consumeWithConfig('key-y', 1, 60000);\n\n      await strategy.reset('key-x');\n\n      const resultX = await strategy.consumeWithConfig('key-x', 1, 60000);\n      const resultY = await strategy.consumeWithConfig('key-y', 1, 60000);\n\n      expect(resultX.allowed).toBe(true); // Reset\n      expect(resultY.allowed).toBe(false); // Still blocked\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 4. Get Current State\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('get()', () => {\n    it('should return null for unknown key', async () => {\n      const result = await strategy.get('nonexistent:key');\n      expect(result).toBeNull();\n    });\n\n    it('should return current state without consuming a point', async () => {\n      const key = 'get:key';\n      await strategy.consumeWithConfig(key, 3, 60000);\n\n      const state = await strategy.get(key);\n      expect(state).not.toBeNull();\n      expect(state!.remaining).toBe(4); // 1 consumed out of default limit (5)\n\n      // Get again - remaining should be same (not decremented)\n      const state2 = await strategy.get(key);\n      expect(state2!.remaining).toBe(4);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 5. Cleanup\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Module lifecycle', () => {\n    it('should clean up interval on destroy', () => {\n      const clearIntervalSpy = jest.spyOn(global, 'clearInterval');\n      strategy.onModuleDestroy();\n      expect(clearIntervalSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();\n      clearIntervalSpy.mockRestore();\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":175,"startLine":1,"totalLines":175}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BjyiSVwJh7DFXAVVKrqVHk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { ThrottlerGuard, SlidingWindowStrategy, THROTTLE_KEY, THROTTLE_SKIP_KEY, ThrottleDefaults } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/security';\n2\timport { ExecutionContext, HttpException, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';\n3\timport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\n4\timport { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\n5\timport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\n6\t\n7\tdescribe('ThrottlerGuard', () => {\n8\t  let guard: ThrottlerGuard;\n9\t  let rateLimiter: SlidingWindowStrategy;\n10\t  let reflector: Reflector;\n11\t\n12\t  function createMockContext(overrides?: {\n13\t    user?: { sub?: string; userId?: string; tenantId?: string };\n14\t    ip?: string;\n15\t    headers?: Record<string, string>;\n16\t    isSkip?: boolean;\n17\t    throttleConfig?: Record<string, unknown>;\n18\t  }): ExecutionContext {\n19\t    const request = {\n20\t      user: overrides?.user,\n21\t      ip: overrides?.ip || '127.0.0.1',\n22\t      headers: overrides?.headers || {},\n23\t      connection: { remoteAddress: overrides?.ip || '127.0.0.1' },\n24\t      socket: { remoteAddress: overrides?.ip || '127.0.0.1' },\n25\t      tenantId: overrides?.user?.tenantId,\n26\t    };\n27\t\n28\t    const response = {\n29\t      setHeader: jest.fn(),\n30\t    };\n31\t\n32\t    jest.spyOn(reflector, 'getAllAndOverride').mockImplementation((key: unknown) => {\n33\t      if (key === THROTTLE_SKIP_KEY) return overrides?.isSkip || false;\n34\t      if (key === THROTTLE_KEY) return overrides?.throttleConfig || undefined;\n35\t      return undefined;\n36\t    });\n37\t\n38\t    return {\n39\t      switchToHttp: () => ({\n40\t        getRequest: () => request,\n41\t        getResponse: () => response,\n42\t      }),\n43\t      getHandler: () => ({}),\n44\t      getClass: () => ({}),\n45\t      getType: () => 'http',\n46\t    } as unknown as ExecutionContext;\n47\t  }\n48\t\n49\t  beforeEach(async () => {\n50\t    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n51\t      providers: [\n52\t        ThrottlerGuard,\n53\t        SlidingWindowStrategy,\n54\t        Reflector,\n55\t        {\n56\t          provide: ConfigService,\n57\t          useValue: {\n58\t            get: jest.fn((key: string, defaultValue?: unknown) => {\n59\t              if (key === 'THROTTLE_DEFAULT_LIMIT') return 5; // Low for testing\n60\t              if (key === 'THROTTLE_DEFAULT_TTL') return 60;\n61\t              if (key === 'THROTTLE_ANONYMOUS_LIMIT') return 3;\n62\t              if (key === 'THROTTLE_ENABLED') return true;\n63\t              if (key === 'RATE_LIMIT_DEFAULT') return 100;\n64\t              if (key === 'RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS') return 60000;\n65\t              return defaultValue;\n66\t            }),\n67\t          },\n68\t        },\n69\t      ],\n70\t    }).compile();\n71\t\n72\t    guard = module.get(ThrottlerGuard);\n73\t    rateLimiter = module.get(SlidingWindowStrategy);\n74\t    reflector = module.get(Reflector);\n75\t  });\n76\t\n77\t  afterEach(async () => {\n78\t    // Clean up the sliding window strategy's interval\n79\t    rateLimiter.onModuleDestroy();\n80\t  });\n81\t\n82\t  // ========================================================================\n83\t  // 1. Basic Allow/Block\n84\t  // ========================================================================\n85\t  describe('Basic rate limiting', () => {\n86\t    it('should allow request when under limit', async () => {\n87\t      const context = createMockContext({ user: { sub: 'user-1' } });\n88\t      const result = await guard.canActivate(context);\n89\t      expect(result).toBe(true);\n90\t    });\n91\t\n92\t    it('should block request when limit exceeded', async () => {\n93\t      const context = createMockContext({\n94\t        user: { sub: 'user-block-test' },\n95\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 2, ttl: 60 },\n96\t      });\n97\t\n98\t      // First 2 requests should pass\n99\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n100\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n101\t\n102\t      // Third should be blocked\n103\t      try {\n104\t        await guard.canActivate(context);\n105\t        fail('Should have thrown');\n106\t      } catch (e) {\n107\t        expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(HttpException);\n108\t        expect((e as HttpException).getStatus()).toBe(HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS);\n109\t      }\n110\t    });\n111\t\n112\t    it('should return 429 status with retryAfter in response', async () => {\n113\t      const context = createMockContext({\n114\t        user: { sub: 'user-429-test' },\n115\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n116\t      });\n117\t\n118\t      await guard.canActivate(context);\n119\t\n120\t      try {\n121\t        await guard.canActivate(context);\n122\t        fail('Should have thrown');\n123\t      } catch (e) {\n124\t        const response = (e as HttpException).getResponse() as Record<string, unknown>;\n125\t        expect(response['statusCode']).toBe(429);\n126\t        expect(response['retryAfter']).toBeDefined();\n127\t        expect(typeof response['retryAfter']).toBe('number');\n128\t      }\n129\t    });\n130\t  });\n131\t\n132\t  // ========================================================================\n133\t  // 2. Skip Throttle\n134\t  // ========================================================================\n135\t  describe('@SkipThrottle() decorator', () => {\n136\t    it('should skip throttling when decorator is present', async () => {\n137\t      const context = createMockContext({ isSkip: true });\n138\t\n139\t      // Even with a very low limit, skip should work\n140\t      for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {\n141\t        await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n142\t      }\n143\t    });\n144\t  });\n145\t\n146\t  // ========================================================================\n147\t  // 3. i < 10; i++) {\n175\t        await expect(disabledGuard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n176\t      }\n177\t\n178\t      module.get(SlidingWindowStrategy).onModuleDestroy();\n179\t    });\n180\t\n181\t    it('APA-368: does NOT honour THROTTLE_ENABLED=false in production (the kill switch cannot nullify pre-auth limits)', async () => {\n182\t      // Mock the rate limiter (always blocks) so the guard's isEnabled scoping is\n183\t      // tested in isolation — the real SlidingWindowStrategy fail-fasts in prod\n184\t      // without Redis, which is a separate contract.\n185\t      const alwaysBlocks = {\n186\t        consumeWithConfig: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n187\t          allowed: false,\n188\t          remaining: 0,\n189\t          resetTime: new Date(Date.now() + 60000),\n190\t          retryAfter: 60,\n191\t        }),\n192\t      };\n193\t      const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n194\t        providers: [\n195\t          ThrottlerGuard,\n196\t          { provide: SlidingWindowStrategy, useValue: alwaysBlocks },\n197\t          Reflector,\n198\t          {\n199\t            provide: ConfigService,\n200\t            useValue: {\n201\t              get: jest.fn((key: string, defaultValue?: unknown) => {\n202\t                if (key === 'THROTTLE_ENABLED') return false; // operator tries to disable\n203\t                if (key === 'NODE_ENV') return 'production';\n204\t                return defaultValue;\n205\t              }),\n206\t            },\n207\t          },\n208\t        ],\n209\t      }).compile();\n210\t\n211\t      const prodGuard = module.get(ThrottlerGuard);\n212\t      // Even though THROTTLE_ENABLED=false, production forces enforcement, so the\n213\t      // (always-blocking) limiter runs and a 429 is thrown rather than skipped.\n214\t      await expect(prodGuard.canActivate(createMockContext())).rejects.toThrow(HttpException);\n215\t    });\n216\t  });\n217\t\n218\t  // ========================================================================\n219\t  // 4. Per-User vs Per-IP Rate Limiting\n220\t  // ========================================================================\n221\t  describe('Rate limiting key generation', () => {\n222\t    it('should use separate limits for different users', async () => {\n223\t      const context1 = createMockContext({\n224\t        user: { sub: 'user-a' },\n225\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n226\t      });\n227\t      const context2 = createMockContext({\n228\t        user: { sub: 'user-b' },\n229\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n230\t      });\n231\t\n232\t      // Both should succeed (different users)\n233\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context1)).resolves.toBe(true);\n234\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context2)).resolves.toBe(true);\n235\t    });\n236\t\n237\t    it('should use IP-based limiting for anonymous requests', async () => {\n238\t      const context1 = createMockContext({\n239\t        ip: '192.168.1.1',\n240\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n241\t      });\n242\t      const context2 = createMockContext({\n243\t        ip: '192.168.1.2',\n244\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n245\t expect(guard.canActivate(context2)).resolves.toBe(true);\n250\t    });\n251\t\n252\t    it('should use IP-based limiting when byIp is configured', async () => {\n253\t      const context = createMockContext({\n254\t        user: { sub: 'user-1' },\n255\t        ip: '10.0.0.1',\n256\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60, byIp: true, keyPrefix: 'test-ip' },\n257\t      });\n258\t\n259\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n260\t      // Second request from same IP should fail\n261\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).rejects.toThrow();\n262\t    });\n263\t  });\n264\t\n265\t  // ========================================================================\n266\t  // 5. Rate Limit Headers\n267\t  // ========================================================================\n268\t  describe('Rate limit response headers', () => {\n269\t    it('should set X-RateLimit headers on response', async () => {\n270\t      const context = createMockContext({\n271\t        user: { sub: 'header-test-user' },\n272\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 10, ttl: 60 },\n273\t      });\n274\t\n275\t      await guard.canActivate(context);\n276\t\n277\t      const response = context.switchToHttp().getResponse();\n278\t      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Limit', '10');\n279\t      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Remaining', expect.any(String));\n280\t      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Reset', expect.any(String));\n281\t      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Policy', '10;w=60');\n282\t    });\n283\t\n284\t    it('should show decreasing remaining count', async () => {\n285\t      const context = createMockContext({\n286\t        user: { sub: 'decrement-test' },\n287\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 3, ttl: 60 },\n288\t      });\n289\t\n290\t      await guard.canActivate(context);\n291\t      const response = context.switchToHttp().getResponse();\n292\t      const remainingCalls = () =>\n293\t        (response.setHeader as jest.Mock).mock.calls.filter(\n294\t          (c: unknown[]) => c[0] === 'X-RateLimit-Remaining',\n295\t        );\n296\t\n297\t      const firstRemaining = parseInt(\n298\t        remainingCalls().at(-1)?.[1] || '0',\n299\t      );\n300\t\n301\t      await guard.canActivate(context);\n302\t      const secondRemaining = parseInt(\n303\t        remainingCalls().at(-1)?.[1] || '0',\n304\t      );\n305\t\n306\t      expect(secondRemaining).toBeLessThan(firstRemaining);\n307\t    });\n308\t  });\n309\t\n310\t  // ========================================================================\n311\t  // 6. Custom Throttle Configurations\n312\t  // ========================================================================\n313\t  describe('Custom throttle configurations', () => {\n314\t    it('should use ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN config (5 per 15min, IP-based)', () => {\n315\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN.limit).toBe(5);\n316\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN.ttl).toBe(900);\n317\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN.byIp).toBe(true);\n318\t    });\n319\t\n320\t    it('should use ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET config (3 per hour, IP-based)', () => {\n321\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET.limit).toBe(3);\n322\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET.ttl).toBe(3600);\n323\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET.byIp).toBe(true);\n324\t    });\n325\t\n326\t    it('should use ThrottleDefaults.SENSITIVE config (3 per 5min)', () => {\n327\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.SENSITIVE.limit).toBe(3);\n328\t      expect(ThrottleDefaults.SENSITIVE.ttl).toBe(300);\n329\t    });\n330\t\n331\t    it('should use custom error message from decorator config', async () => {\n332\t      const context = createMockContext({\n333\t        user: { sub: 'custom-msg-user' },\n334\t        throttleConfig: {\n335\t          limit: 1,\n336\t          ttl: 60,\n337\t          errorMessage: 'Custom: Too many login attempts',\n338\t        },\n339\t      });\n340\t\n341\t      await guard.canActivate(context);\n342\t\n343\t      try {\n344\t        await guard.canActivate(context);\n345\t        fail('Should have thrown');\n346\t      } catch (e) {\n347\t        const response = (e as HttpException).getResponse() as Record<string, unknown>;\n348\t        expect(response['message']).toBe('Custom: Too many login attempts');\n349\t      }\n350\t    });\n351\t  });\n352\t\n353\t  // ========================================================================\n354\t  // 7. IP Extraction\n355\t  // ========================================================================\n356\t  describe('IP extraction and validation', () => {\n357\t    it('should fall back to unknown-ip for invalid IPs', async () => {\n358\t      const context = createMockContext({\n359\t        ip: 'not-an-ip',\n360\t        headers: {},\n361\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n362\t      });\n363\t\n364\t      // Should still work (using fallback IP)\n365\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n366\t    });\n367\t\n368\t    it('should handle IPv6 addresses', async () => {\n369\t      const context = createMockContext({\n370\t        ip: '::ffff:192.168.1.1',\n371\t        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n372\t      });\n373\t\n374\t      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n375\t    });\n376\t  });\n377\t});\n378\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"837fae49-0fef-4520-8528-cd9d4bf55882","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:20:19.925Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/security/throttler-guard.spec.ts","content":"import { ThrottlerGuard, SlidingWindowStrategy, THROTTLE_KEY, THROTTLE_SKIP_KEY, ThrottleDefaults } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/security';\nimport { ExecutionContext, HttpException, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\n\ndescribe('ThrottlerGuard', () => {\n  let guard: ThrottlerGuard;\n  let rateLimiter: SlidingWindowStrategy;\n  let reflector: Reflector;\n\n  function createMockContext(overrides?: {\n    user?: { sub?: string; userId?: string; tenantId?: string };\n    ip?: string;\n    headers?: Record<string, string>;\n    isSkip?: boolean;\n    throttleConfig?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  }): ExecutionContext {\n    const request = {\n      user: overrides?.user,\n      ip: overrides?.ip || '127.0.0.1',\n      headers: overrides?.headers || {},\n      connection: { remoteAddress: overrides?.ip || '127.0.0.1' },\n      socket: { remoteAddress: overrides?.ip || '127.0.0.1' },\n      tenantId: overrides?.user?.tenantId,\n    };\n\n    const response = {\n      setHeader: jest.fn(),\n    };\n\n    jest.spyOn(reflector, 'getAllAndOverride').mockImplementation((key: unknown) => {\n      if (key === THROTTLE_SKIP_KEY) return overrides?.isSkip || false;\n      if (key === THROTTLE_KEY) return overrides?.throttleConfig || undefined;\n      return undefined;\n    });\n\n    return {\n      switchToHttp: () => ({\n        getRequest: () => request,\n        getResponse: () => response,\n      }),\n      getHandler: () => ({}),\n      getClass: () => ({}),\n      getType: () => 'http',\n    } as unknown as ExecutionContext;\n  }\n\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      providers: [\n        ThrottlerGuard,\n        SlidingWindowStrategy,\n        Reflector,\n        {\n          provide: ConfigService,\n          useValue: {\n            get: jest.fn((key: string, defaultValue?: unknown) => {\n              if (key === 'THROTTLE_DEFAULT_LIMIT') return 5; // Low for testing\n              if (key === 'THROTTLE_DEFAULT_TTL') return 60;\n              if (key === 'THROTTLE_ANONYMOUS_LIMIT') return 3;\n              if (key === 'THROTTLE_ENABLED') return true;\n              if (key === 'RATE_LIMIT_DEFAULT') return 100;\n              if (key === 'RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS') return 60000;\n              return defaultValue;\n            }),\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    }).compile();\n\n    guard = module.get(ThrottlerGuard);\n    rateLimiter = module.get(SlidingWindowStrategy);\n    reflector = module.get(Reflector);\n  });\n\n  afterEach(async () => {\n    // Clean up the sliding window strategy's interval\n    rateLimiter.onModuleDestroy();\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 1. Basic Allow/Block\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Basic rate limiting', () => {\n    it('should allow request when under limit', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({ user: { sub: 'user-1' } });\n      const result = await guard.canActivate(context);\n      expect(result).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should block request when limit exceeded', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'user-block-test' },\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 2, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      // First 2 requests should pass\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n\n      // Third should be blocked\n      try {\n        await guard.canActivate(context);\n        fail('Should have thrown');\n      } catch (e) {\n        expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(HttpException);\n        expect((e as HttpException).getStatus()).toBe(HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS);\n      }\n    });\n\n    it('should return 429 status with retryAfter in response', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'user-429-test' },\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      await guard.canActivate(context);\n\n      try {\n        await guard.canActivate(context);\n        fail('Should have thrown');\n      } catch (e) {\n        const response = (e as HttpException).getResponse() as Record<string, unknown>;\n        expect(response['statusCode']).toBe(429);\n        expect(response['retryAfter']).toBeDefined();\n        expect(typeof response['retryAfter']).toBe('number');\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 2. Skip Throttle\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('@SkipThrottle() decorator', () => {\n    it('should skip throttling when decorator is present', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({ isSkip: true });\n\n      // Even with a very low limit, skip should work\n      for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {\n        await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 3. Disabled Throttling\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Disabled throttling', () => {\n    it('should allow all requests when throttling is disabled', async () => {\n      const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n        providers: [\n          ThrottlerGuard,\n          SlidingWindowStrategy,\n          Reflector,\n          {\n            provide: ConfigService,\n            useValue: {\n              get: jest.fn((key: string, defaultValue?: unknown) => {\n                if (key === 'THROTTLE_ENABLED') return false;\n                if (key === 'RATE_LIMIT_DEFAULT') return 1;\n                if (key === 'RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS') return 60000;\n                return defaultValue ?? 1;\n              }),\n            },\n          },\n        ],\n      }).compile();\n\n      const disabledGuard = module.get(ThrottlerGuard);\n      const context = createMockContext();\n\n      // Should always pass even with limit=1\n      for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n        await expect(disabledGuard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n      }\n\n      module.get(SlidingWindowStrategy).onModuleDestroy();\n    });\n\n    it('APA-368: does NOT honour THROTTLE_ENABLED=false in production (the kill switch cannot nullify pre-auth limits)', async () => {\n      // Mock the rate limiter (always blocks) so the guard's isEnabled scoping is\n      // tested in isolation — the real SlidingWindowStrategy fail-fasts in prod\n      // without Redis, which is a separate contract.\n      const alwaysBlocks = {\n        consumeWithConfig: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({\n          allowed: false,\n          remaining: 0,\n          resetTime: new Date(Date.now() + 60000),\n          retryAfter: 60,\n        }),\n      };\n      const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n        providers: [\n          ThrottlerGuard,\n          { provide: SlidingWindowStrategy, useValue: alwaysBlocks },\n          Reflector,\n          {\n            provide: ConfigService,\n            useValue: {\n              get: jest.fn((key: string, defaultValue?: unknown) => {\n                if (key === 'THROTTLE_ENABLED') return false; // operator tries to disable\n                if (key === 'NODE_ENV') return 'production';\n                return defaultValue;\n              }),\n            },\n          },\n        ],\n      }).compile();\n\n      const prodGuard = module.get(ThrottlerGuard);\n      // Even though THROTTLE_ENABLED=false, production forces enforcement, so the\n      // (always-blocking) limiter runs and a 429 is thrown rather than skipped.\n      await expect(prodGuard.canActivate(createMockContext())).rejects.toThrow(HttpException);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 4. Per-User vs Per-IP Rate Limiting\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Rate limiting key generation', () => {\n    it('should use separate limits for different users', async () => {\n      const context1 = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'user-a' },\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n      const context2 = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'user-b' },\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      // Both should succeed (different users)\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context1)).resolves.toBe(true);\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context2)).resolves.toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should use IP-based limiting for anonymous requests', async () => {\n      const context1 = createMockContext({\n        ip: '192.168.1.1',\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n      const context2 = createMockContext({\n        ip: '192.168.1.2',\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      // Different IPs should have separate limits\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context1)).resolves.toBe(true);\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context2)).resolves.toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should use IP-based limiting when byIp is configured', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'user-1' },\n        ip: '10.0.0.1',\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60, byIp: true, keyPrefix: 'test-ip' },\n      });\n\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n      // Second request from same IP should fail\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).rejects.toThrow();\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 5. Rate Limit Headers\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Rate limit response headers', () => {\n    it('should set X-RateLimit headers on response', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'header-test-user' },\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 10, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      await guard.canActivate(context);\n\n      const response = context.switchToHttp().getResponse();\n      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Limit', '10');\n      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Remaining', expect.any(String));\n      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Reset', expect.any(String));\n      expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('X-RateLimit-Policy', '10;w=60');\n    });\n\n    it('should show decreasing remaining count', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'decrement-test' },\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 3, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      await guard.canActivate(context);\n      const response = context.switchToHttp().getResponse();\n      const remainingCalls = () =>\n        (response.setHeader as jest.Mock).mock.calls.filter(\n          (c: unknown[]) => c[0] === 'X-RateLimit-Remaining',\n        );\n\n      const firstRemaining = parseInt(\n        remainingCalls().at(-1)?.[1] || '0',\n      );\n\n      await guard.canActivate(context);\n      const secondRemaining = parseInt(\n        remainingCalls().at(-1)?.[1] || '0',\n      );\n\n      expect(secondRemaining).toBeLessThan(firstRemaining);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 6. Custom Throttle Configurations\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('Custom throttle configurations', () => {\n    it('should use ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN config (5 per 15min, IP-based)', () => {\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN.limit).toBe(5);\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN.ttl).toBe(900);\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.LOGIN.byIp).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should use ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET config (3 per hour, IP-based)', () => {\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET.limit).toBe(3);\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET.ttl).toBe(3600);\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.PASSWORD_RESET.byIp).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should use ThrottleDefaults.SENSITIVE config (3 per 5min)', () => {\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.SENSITIVE.limit).toBe(3);\n      expect(ThrottleDefaults.SENSITIVE.ttl).toBe(300);\n    });\n\n    it('should use custom error message from decorator config', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        user: { sub: 'custom-msg-user' },\n        throttleConfig: {\n          limit: 1,\n          ttl: 60,\n          errorMessage: 'Custom: Too many login attempts',\n        },\n      });\n\n      await guard.canActivate(context);\n\n      try {\n        await guard.canActivate(context);\n        fail('Should have thrown');\n      } catch (e) {\n        const response = (e as HttpException).getResponse() as Record<string, unknown>;\n        expect(response['message']).toBe('Custom: Too many login attempts');\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  // ========================================================================\n  // 7. IP Extraction\n  // ========================================================================\n  describe('IP extraction and validation', () => {\n    it('should fall back to unknown-ip for invalid IPs', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        ip: 'not-an-ip',\n        headers: {},\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      // Should still work (using fallback IP)\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it('should handle IPv6 addresses', async () => {\n      const context = createMockContext({\n        ip: '::ffff:192.168.1.1',\n        throttleConfig: { limit: 1, ttl: 60 },\n      });\n\n      await expect(guard.canActivate(context)).resolves.toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":378,"startLine":1,"totalLines":378}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SpToGX423tx5tzoViBcM52","type":"tool_result","content":"2454\tdef _extract_envelope_json(text: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n2455\t    \"\"\"Find the agent's embedded envelope JSON in a natural-language reply.\n2456\t\n2457\t    Scan order (first match wins):\n2458\t      1. Fenced ```json``` blocks containing a JSON object.\n2459\t      2. Last balanced top-level {...} block in the text.\n2460\t\n2461\t    Returns the parsed dict or None when no JSON is recoverable.\n2462\t    \"\"\"\n2463\t    # Pass 1: fenced JSON blocks — prefer the LAST one (agents typically\n2464\t    # narrate first then close with the envelope).\n2465\t    matches = _FENCED_JSON_RE.findall(text)\n2466\t    for body in reversed(matches):\n2467\t        try:\n2468\t            data = json.loads(body)\n2469\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n2470\t            continue\n2471\t        if isinstance(data, dict):\n2472\t            return data\n2473\t    # Pass 2: balanced-brace scan from end-of-text for the last {...} block.\n2474\t    depth = 0\n2475\t    end_idx = -1\n2476\t    for i in range(len(text) - 1, -1, -1):\n2477\t        ch = text[i]\n2478\t        if ch == \"}\":\n2479\t            if depth == 0:\n2480\t                end_idx = i\n2481\t            depth += 1\n2482\t        elif ch == \"{\":\n2483\t            depth -= 1\n2484\t            if depth == 0 and end_idx > 0:\n2485\t                candidate = text[i : end_idx + 1]\n2486\t                try:\n2487\t                    data = json.loads(candidate)\n2488\t                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n2489\t                    end_idx = -1\n2490\t                    continue\n2491\t                if isinstance(data, dict):\n2492\t                    return data\n2493\t    return None\n2494\t\n2495\t\n2496\tdef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2497\t    *,\n2498\t    raw_stdout: str,\n2499\t    request_id: str,\n2500\t    claim_id: str,\n2501\t    agent_id: str,\n2502\t    role: str,\n2503\t    subagent_type: str,\n2504\t    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2505\t    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n2506\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n2507\t    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n2508\t\n2509\t    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n2510\t    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n2511\t    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n2512\t    \"\"\"\n2513\t    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n2514\t    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n2515\t    if not agent_text:\n2516\t        agent_text = raw_stdout\n2517\t    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n2518\t\n2519\t    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n2520\t        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2521\t        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2522\t        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2523\t        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2524\t        \"role\": role,\n2525\t        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2526\t    }\n2527\t\n2528\t    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2529\t    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2530\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2531\t    else:\n2532\t        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2533\t        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n2534\t        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n2535\t        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n2536\t        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n2537\t        # the reply.\n2538\t        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2539\t        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n2540\t        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n2541\t        if must_satisfy:\n2542\t            for item in must_satisfy:\n2543\t                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n2544\t                    continue\n2545\t                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n2546\t                if not cid:\n2547\t                    continue\n2548\t                synthesized.append({\n2549\t                    \"id\": cid,\n2550\t                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2551\t                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2552\t                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2553\t                })\n2554\t        if not synthesized:\n2555\t            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n2556\t            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n2557\t            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n2558\t            synthesized.append({\n2559\t                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n2560\t                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2561\t                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2562\t                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2563\t            })"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"6abc4768-4316-4cd7-bc85-2c9771056eeb","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:24:36.432Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"def _extract_envelope_json(text: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Find the agent's embedded envelope JSON in a natural-language reply.\n\n    Scan order (first match wins):\n      1. Fenced ```json``` blocks containing a JSON object.\n      2. Last balanced top-level {...} block in the text.\n\n    Returns the parsed dict or None when no JSON is recoverable.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pass 1: fenced JSON blocks — prefer the LAST one (agents typically\n    # narrate first then close with the envelope).\n    matches = _FENCED_JSON_RE.findall(text)\n    for body in reversed(matches):\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(body)\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n            continue\n        if isinstance(data, dict):\n            return data\n    # Pass 2: balanced-brace scan from end-of-text for the last {...} block.\n    depth = 0\n    end_idx = -1\n    for i in range(len(text) - 1, -1, -1):\n        ch = text[i]\n        if ch == \"}\":\n            if depth == 0:\n                end_idx = i\n            depth += 1\n        elif ch == \"{\":\n            depth -= 1\n            if depth == 0 and end_idx > 0:\n                candidate = text[i : end_idx + 1]\n                try:\n                    data = json.loads(candidate)\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                    end_idx = -1\n                    continue\n                if isinstance(data, dict):\n                    return data\n    return None\n\n\ndef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n    *,\n    raw_stdout: str,\n    request_id: str,\n    claim_id: str,\n    agent_id: str,\n    role: str,\n    subagent_type: str,\n    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n\n    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n    \"\"\"\n    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n    if not agent_text:\n        agent_text = raw_stdout\n    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n\n    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n        \"request_id\": request_id,\n        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"role\": role,\n        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n    }\n\n    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n    else:\n        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n        # the reply.\n        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n        if must_satisfy:\n            for item in must_satisfy:\n                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n                    continue\n                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n                if not cid:\n                    continue\n                synthesized.append({\n                    \"id\": cid,\n                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n                })\n        if not synthesized:\n            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n            synthesized.append({\n                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n            })","numLines":110,"startLine":2454,"totalLines":5265}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YLtAic8EnhPsVowJ3UuY6y","type":"tool_result","content":"2564\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n2565\t\n2566\t    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n2567\t    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n2568\t        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n2569\t            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n2570\t    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n2571\t        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n2572\t\n2573\t    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n2574\t    # forensic context post-submission.\n2575\t    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n2576\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n2577\t        details = {}\n2578\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n2579\t    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n2580\t    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n2581\t    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n2582\t    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n2583\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n2584\t    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n2585\t    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n2586\t    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n2587\t    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n2588\t    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n2589\t    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n2590\t    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n2591\t    if dispatch_model:\n2592\t        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n2593\t    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n2594\t    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n2595\t    if usage is not None:\n2596\t        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n2597\t    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n2598\t\n2599\t    return envelope\n2600\t\n2601\t\n2602\tdef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n2603\t    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n2604\t\n2605\t    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n2606\t    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n2607\t    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n2608\t    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n2609\t    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else\n2610\t    on a non-zero exit is a refusal before the row and leaves the claim live.\n2611\t    \"\"\"\n2612\t    text = submit_stdout or \"\"\n2613\t    start = text.find(\"{\")\n2614\t    if start < 0:\n2615\t        return False\n2616\t    try:\n2617\t        payload = json.loads(text[start:])\n2618\t    except ValueError:\n2619\t        return False\n2620\t    row = payload.get(\"row\") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n2621\t    return (isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\"\n2622\t            and isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"row_type\") == \"result\" and row.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\")\n2623\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"ba55fb0a-eedd-4ae7-83ae-e35ee52caec1","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:24:42.608Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n\n    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n\n    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n    # forensic context post-submission.\n    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n    if dispatch_model:\n        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n    if usage is not None:\n        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n\n    return envelope\n\n\ndef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n\n    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else\n    on a non-zero exit is a refusal before the row and leaves the claim live.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = submit_stdout or \"\"\n    start = text.find(\"{\")\n    if start < 0:\n        return False\n    try:\n        payload = json.loads(text[start:])\n    except ValueError:\n        return False\n    row = payload.get(\"row\") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n    return (isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\"\n            and isinstance(row, dict) and row.get(\"row_type\") == \"result\" and row.get(\"status\") == \"rejected\")\n","numLines":60,"startLine":2564,"totalLines":5265}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J7K2SsFrc8tgdhUejzg6js","type":"tool_result","content":"380\t# Unaltered-by-construction beats repo-committed here; a tampered or\n381\t# invented artifact fails.\n382\tARBITRATION_ROLES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n383\t    \"consensus_arbitration\",\n384\t    \"human_required_adjudication\",\n385\t})\n386\t\n387\t\n388\tdef _artifact_results_ledger(artifact: Path) -> Path | None:\n389\t    \"\"\"The results ledger that owns an outputs/ artifact, derived from the\n390\t    artifact itself.\n391\t\n392\t    Deriving beats configuration here: the store lives at different roots\n393\t    on the runner, in the operator clone and in a test fixture, and the\n394\t    ledger is ALWAYS `<…>/agent-invocations/results.jsonl` beside the\n395\t    `outputs/` tree the artifact sits in. Asking a tools-dir resolver\n396\t    instead would make admissibility depend on ambient environment.\n397\t    \"\"\"\n398\t    for parent in artifact.parents:\n399\t        if parent.name == \"agent-invocations\":\n400\t            if \"outputs\" not in artifact.relative_to(parent).parts[:1]:\n401\t                return None\n402\t            return parent / \"results.jsonl\"\n403\t    return None\n404\t\n405\t\n406\tdef _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref: str, root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n407\t    \"\"\"(admissible, reason) for a ref naming a kernel-recorded agent output.\n408\t\n409\t    Admissible only when the results ledger beside the artifact records\n410\t    it AND the bytes still hash to the recorded value: provenance by\n411\t    content address, which a fabricated or edited artifact cannot forge.\n412\t    \"\"\"\n413\t    import hashlib\n414\t\n415\t    raw = ref\n416\t    if ref.count(\":\") >= 1 and ref.rsplit(\":\", 1)[-1].isdigit():\n417\t        raw = ref.rsplit(\":\", 1)[0]\n418\t    candidate = Path(raw) if Path(raw).is_absolute() else (root / raw)\n419\t    try:\n420\t        candidate = candidate.resolve()\n421\t    except OSError:\n422\t        return False, \"artifact_unresolvable\"\n423\t    ledger = _artifact_results_ledger(candidate)\n424\t    if ledger is None:\n425\t        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n426\t    if not ledger.exists():\n427\t        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n428\t    recorded: str | None = None\n429\t    for row in load_declared_jsonl(ledger, expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_results\"):\n430\t        output_path = row.get(\"output_path\")\n431\t        output_hash = row.get(\"output_hash\")\n432\t        if not isinstance(output_path, str) or not isinstance(output_hash, str):\n433\t            continue\n434\t        # Rows carry both spellings in the wild: Y6 made new rows\n435\t        # store-relative; older rows (and the live drain that surfaced\n436\t        # this defect) carry the runner's absolute path. Compare by the\n437\t        # file named, never by how the row spelled it.\n438\t        try:\n439\t            row_path = Path(output_path)\n440\t            resolved = (\n441\t                row_path.resolve()\n442\t                if row_path.is_absolute()\n443\t                else (ledger.parent.parent / output_path).resolve()\n444\t            )\n445\t        except OSError:\n446\t            continue\n447\t        if resolved == candidate:\n448\t            recorded = output_hash\n449\t            break\n450\t    if recorded is None:\n451\t        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n452\t    if not candidate.exists() or not candidate.is_file():\n453\t        return False, \"artifact_missing\"\n454\t    digest = \"sha256:\" + hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n455\t    if digest != recorded:\n456\t        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n457\t    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n458\t\n459\t\n460\tdef _check_agent_ref(\n461\t    ref: str,\n462\t    *,\n463\t    root: Path,\n464\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n465\t    checked: list[str],\n466\t    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n467\t    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n468\t) -> None:\n469\t    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n470\t    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n471\t    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n472\t    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n473\t    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n474\t    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n475\t    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n476\t    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n477\t    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n478\t    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n479\t    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n480\t    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n481\t        checked.append(ref)\n482\t        return\n483\t    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n484\t        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n485\t        if admissible:\n486\t            checked.append(ref)\n487\t            return\n488\t        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n489\t            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n490\t            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n491\t            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n492\t            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n493\t            errors.append({\n494\t                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n495\t                \"ref\": ref,\n496\t                \"reason\": reason,\n497\t            })\n498\t            return\n499\t    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n500\t    if parsed is None:\n501\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n502\t        return\n503\t    path, line = parsed\n504\t    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n505\t    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n506\t    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n507\t    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n508\t    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n509\t    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n510\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n511\t    try:\n512\t        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n513\t    except _GE as exc:\n514\t        msg = str(exc)\n515\t        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n516\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n517\t        else:\n518\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n519\t                           \"error\": msg})\n520\t        return\n521\t    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n522\t    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n523\t    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n524\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n525\t        return\n526\t    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n527\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n528\t        return\n529\t    checked.append(rel_str)\n530\t    if line is None:\n531\t        return\n532\t    if line <= 0:\n533\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n534\t        return\n535\t    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n536\t    if line > line_count:\n537\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n538\t\n539\t\n540\tdef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n541\t    *,\n542\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n543\t    workspace_root: str | Path,\n544\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n545\t    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n546\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n547\t    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n548\t\n549\t    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n550\t    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n551\t    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n552\t    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n553\t    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n554\t    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n555\t    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n556\t\n557\t    Returns: {\n558\t        \"valid\": bool,\n559\t        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n560\t        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n561\t    }\n562\t    \"\"\"\n563\t    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n564\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n565\t    checked: list[str] = []\n566\t    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n567\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n568\t    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n569\t    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n570\t    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n571\t        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n572\t    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n573\t    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n574\t    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n575\t    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n576\t    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n577\t    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n578\t    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n579\t    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"faadac61-ad5c-4c03-999b-208448757c08","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:24:56.877Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"# Unaltered-by-construction beats repo-committed here; a tampered or\n# invented artifact fails.\nARBITRATION_ROLES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n    \"consensus_arbitration\",\n    \"human_required_adjudication\",\n})\n\n\ndef _artifact_results_ledger(artifact: Path) -> Path | None:\n    \"\"\"The results ledger that owns an outputs/ artifact, derived from the\n    artifact itself.\n\n    Deriving beats configuration here: the store lives at different roots\n    on the runner, in the operator clone and in a test fixture, and the\n    ledger is ALWAYS `<…>/agent-invocations/results.jsonl` beside the\n    `outputs/` tree the artifact sits in. Asking a tools-dir resolver\n    instead would make admissibility depend on ambient environment.\n    \"\"\"\n    for parent in artifact.parents:\n        if parent.name == \"agent-invocations\":\n            if \"outputs\" not in artifact.relative_to(parent).parts[:1]:\n                return None\n            return parent / \"results.jsonl\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref: str, root: Path) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"(admissible, reason) for a ref naming a kernel-recorded agent output.\n\n    Admissible only when the results ledger beside the artifact records\n    it AND the bytes still hash to the recorded value: provenance by\n    content address, which a fabricated or edited artifact cannot forge.\n    \"\"\"\n    import hashlib\n\n    raw = ref\n    if ref.count(\":\") >= 1 and ref.rsplit(\":\", 1)[-1].isdigit():\n        raw = ref.rsplit(\":\", 1)[0]\n    candidate = Path(raw) if Path(raw).is_absolute() else (root / raw)\n    try:\n        candidate = candidate.resolve()\n    except OSError:\n        return False, \"artifact_unresolvable\"\n    ledger = _artifact_results_ledger(candidate)\n    if ledger is None:\n        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n    if not ledger.exists():\n        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n    recorded: str | None = None\n    for row in load_declared_jsonl(ledger, expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_results\"):\n        output_path = row.get(\"output_path\")\n        output_hash = row.get(\"output_hash\")\n        if not isinstance(output_path, str) or not isinstance(output_hash, str):\n            continue\n        # Rows carry both spellings in the wild: Y6 made new rows\n        # store-relative; older rows (and the live drain that surfaced\n        # this defect) carry the runner's absolute path. Compare by the\n        # file named, never by how the row spelled it.\n        try:\n            row_path = Path(output_path)\n            resolved = (\n                row_path.resolve()\n                if row_path.is_absolute()\n                else (ledger.parent.parent / output_path).resolve()\n            )\n        except OSError:\n            continue\n        if resolved == candidate:\n            recorded = output_hash\n            break\n    if recorded is None:\n        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n    if not candidate.exists() or not candidate.is_file():\n        return False, \"artifact_missing\"\n    digest = \"sha256:\" + hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    if digest != recorded:\n        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n\n\ndef _check_agent_ref(\n    ref: str,\n    *,\n    root: Path,\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checked: list[str],\n    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n        checked.append(ref)\n        return\n    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n        if admissible:\n            checked.append(ref)\n            return\n        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n            })\n            return\n    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n    if parsed is None:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n        return\n    path, line = parsed\n    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n    try:\n        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n    except _GE as exc:\n        msg = str(exc)\n        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n        else:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n                           \"error\": msg})\n        return\n    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n        return\n    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n        return\n    checked.append(rel_str)\n    if line is None:\n        return\n    if line <= 0:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n        return\n    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n    if line > line_count:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n\n\ndef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n    *,\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    workspace_root: str | Path,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n\n    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n\n    Returns: {\n        \"valid\": bool,\n        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    checked: list[str] = []\n    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being","numLines":200,"startLine":380,"totalLines":774}}}

Refs\n673\t    # outside the box are rejected as scope leakage.\n674\t    #\n675\t    # Plan 024 §B-2 — allowed_scope MUST be provided when a request is\n676\t    # supplied. present.\n683\t    if request is not None:\n684\t        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n685\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n686\t        else:\n687\t            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n688\t            allowed_request_refs = {\n689\t                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n690\t                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n691\t                if isinstance(r, str)\n692\t            }\n693\t            for path in checked:\n694\t                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n695\t                    continue  # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n699\t                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n700\t                    continue\n701\t                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n702\t                    continue\n703\t                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n704\t                    errors.append(\n705\t                        {\n706\t                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n707\t                            \"path\": path,\n708\t                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n709\t                        }"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e2cca21f-c9ce-4921-a66e-9bd1f565f6f8","uuid":"f8e5c3d1-a206-4177-ba5d-a7cd4c2fd6cf","timestamp":"2026-09-21T01:25:00.805Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-28494b3f855a/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n\n    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n        response_refs = []\n    for ref in response_refs:\n        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n            continue\n        _check_agent_ref(\n            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n        )\n        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n            continue  # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n            continue\n        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n            ref,\n            workspace_root=root,\n            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n            probe_session=probe_session,\n        )\n        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n        try:\n            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n        except GovernanceError as exc:\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": str(exc),\n            })\n\n    # Combined with the empty-allowed_scope skip (line below), a judge\n    # could rubber-stamp a request with zero criteria checked. The\n    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n        for entry in matrix:\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                continue\n            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n                continue\n            for ref in entry_refs:\n                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n                    )\n                    continue\n                _check_agent_ref(\n                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n                )\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n                    continue  # Pre-fix the empty-list case skipped scope enforcement\n    # entirely under \"preserves backward compatibility with legacy\n    # aria/agent-invocation-request/v1 rows that pre-date Plan 016\".\n    # That path is now closed: legacy rows are caught at\n    # _strict_request_view (line ~964); by the time validate_agent_-\n    # response_evidence sees the request the strict fields are\n    # present.\n    if request is not None:\n        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n        else:\n            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n            allowed_request_refs = {\n                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n                if isinstance(r, str)\n            }\n            for path in checked:\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n                    continue  # Z2 — pointer identity is bound at mint, not by glob\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(path, root)[0]:\n                    # ORPHAN-734 — an arbitrated artifact's identity is bound\n                    # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n                    continue\n                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n                    continue\n                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\n                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n                            \"path\": path,\n                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n                        }","numLines":130,"startLine":580,"totalLines":774}}}
